The instructions continue: Since it is sometimes necessary to write with colored inks, you should have several ink-wells <note>original: horns</note> containing white ink, into which you can infuse your colors for the pentacles and symbols <note>original: characters</note>. These colors must be diluted with the blessed water that we call "exorcised" and with gum arabic <note>A natural resin used as a binder in ink and paint</note>. Finally, perfume them with incense <note>original: fumigate</note>. This must be done with complete devotion, humility, and faith, for without these, the *Grimoire* says nothing can be accomplished.

The same work gives details regarding how to pluck a "sword-feather" from the right wing of a swallow, saying the words *Yin He* before doing so. It should then be fashioned into a pen using the Knife of the Art <note>A specialized ritual knife used to create magical tools</note>, and the name *Anereton* must be written on it with a goose-quill. After this, Psalm 133, "Behold, how good and how pleasant," <note>original: *Ecce quam bonum et quam jucundum*</note> and Psalm 117, "Praise the Lord, all you nations," <note>original: *Laudate Dominum omnes gentes*</note> are to be recited over it, followed by sprinkling with holy water <note>original: aspersion</note> and perfuming with incense <note>original: fumigation</note>.

You should not assume that ordinary ink would be suitable for such a serious task as drafting formal pacts <note>original: engrossing pacts</note>. Even the consecrations mentioned earlier are insufficient for this purpose. While it is understood that the signature is written with the practitioner's blood, the deed itself requires a special preparation, as follows: Place river water in a new, glazed <note>original: varnished</note> earthenware pot along with the powder described below. Then take sprigs of fern gathered on St. John's Eve <note>June 23rd, a night traditionally associated with the gathering of magical herbs</note> and vine twigs cut during the full moon of March. Light this wood using virgin paper <note>Paper that has never been used for any other purpose</note>, and when the water boils, the ink will be ready. A new batch must be made every time you need to write—that is, whenever you intend to summon <note>original: appellation</note> a spirit.

->*Powder for Pact-Ink*<-

| | |
| :--- | :--- |
| Gall-nuts <note>A source of tannin used to make traditional iron gall ink</note> | 10 Oz. |
| Roman Vitriol or Green Copperas <note>Iron sulfate, which reacts with tannin to create black pigment</note> | 3 Oz. |
| Rock Alum or Gum Arabic | 3 Oz. |

Make this into a very fine <note>original: impalpable</note> powder, and use it as described above when you wish to create the ink.

The authorities for this process are not the primary *Grimoires* themselves, but rather the collections of "Magical Secrets" that are sometimes attached to them or exist as independent works.

### § 5. Concerning Virgin Wax or Virgin Earth 1

The candles used in summoning spirits <note>original: evocation</note> or in other magical rituals, and the figurines required for casting spells <note>original: bewitchment</note>, must be made of "virgin wax" or "virgin earth"—meaning these substances must never have been used for any other purpose before. 2 Before beginning the work, the following words must be recited over the material: ENTABOR, NATABOR, *If I shall be silent* <note>original: Si tacibor</note>, ADONAY, AN, LAYAMON, TINARMES, EOS PHILODES. 3 *Angels of God, be present! I call upon you in my work, so that I may obtain power <note>original: virtue</note> through your intervention and may be completely successful.* Then recite the Psalms *Lord, my heart is not proud* <note>original: Domine, non est exaltatum cor meum; Psalm 131</note> and *Lord, who shall dwell* <note>original: Domine, quis habitabit; Psalm 15</note>, 4 adding these words: *I exorcise <note>A ritual command to purify or command a spirit or object</note> you, creature of wax [or earth], by the Creator and Almighty God—who created all things from nothing by His Most Holy Name and by His angels—I command you to receive power and a blessing in His Name. May you be sanctified and blessed, obtaining the power we desire through the Most Holy Name ADONAY, which is the life of all creatures, Amen.* Sprinkle <note>original: Asperge</note> the material with the holy water of the Art, then store it away to be used when needed. By doing this, says the book *True Black Magic*, you shall obtain a guaranteed result in everything you undertake. 5

## § 6. Concerning the Silken Cloth <gloss>1</gloss>

When all the instruments belonging to the Art <note>The practice of ceremonial magic</note> have been properly consecrated and purified <note>original: exorcised</note>, they must be gathered into a costly silk cloth. As Solomon ordains—according to the testament—this is done so they may be kept clean and pure, and thus remain more effective <note>original: efficacious</note>. So long as it is not black or brown, the color does not matter <note>original: is indifferent</note>, but the following characters must be written upon it in pigeon's blood using the male goose-quill belonging to the Art.

<image-desc>A horizontal sequence of magical sigils and characters. The symbols include several variations of crosses, glyphs with small circles at their terminals, a square containing another square and a dot, and characters resembling stylized script or astrological signs.</image-desc>

Add these names: ADONAY, AMMASTIUS, ANARETON, COSBOS, ELOYM. Then fumigate with incense and sprinkle with holy water <note>original: asperge</note>, subsequently reciting the Psalms *O Lord, our Lord* <note>original: Domine, Dominus noster; Psalm 8</note>, *Give the King Your Justice, O God* <note>original: Deus Judicium tuum Regi da; Psalm 72</note>, and *Now let Your servant depart* <note>original: Ecce nunc dimittis; the Song of Simeon from Luke 2:29</note>. <gloss>2</gloss> Finally, place all the magical instruments in this silken cloth. "In this way, you shall use them at will and learn their effects," says the Magus, Iroë-Grego. But this promise has a taste of the "salt" which is called common sense <note>original: prudence</note>.

## § 7. Concerning the Victim of the Art

It is usually supposed that the offering of a bloody sacrifice is an essential condition for success in Black Magic, and that the moral degradation <note>original: embrutement; the process of becoming more like an animal or less human through wicked acts</note> of the practitioner is the chief purpose of the ritual instruction. It is true that such an offering is required in most cases, but its object is simple and commonplace rather than diabolical or obscure <note>original: recondite</note>, and the practice itself is based on the so-called White Magic of the *Key of Solomon*. It offers

<page-num>170</page-num>

another instance showing that if this text is the source and fountainhead of the Art <note>The practice of ceremonial magic</note>, it is also the root of its diabolism. The Art—I should explain—has its victim not because blood is required in one case more than another, but because it was customary for pacts and pentacles to be written on parchment or vellum. In view of that doctrine of personal consecration already explained, the Black Magician had to avoid outside labor as much as possible. He had to be his own metalworker <note>original: whitesmith</note>, his own pen-maker, and therefore also his own tanner. "Let no man belong to another who can be his own master" <note>original: "Alterius non sit qui suus esse potest"</note> was the maxim of Paracelsus, and it was carried further in *Goëtia* <note>The practice of conjuring demons</note> than mere intellectual independence. "Make yourself what you want; do not trust it to others; it will not only possess more of your personal power <note>original: virtue</note>, but you will be sure of the quality of the item"—such might be the commentary of the Grimoires upon the aphorism of the sage of Hohenheim <note>A reference to Paracelsus, whose full name was Philippus Aureolus Theophrastus Bombastus von Hohenheim</note>. There is even more reason for this counsel because even the common materials required by the practitioner usually had some difficult conditions attached to them, which might remain unfulfilled unless he himself was actively present during their preparation. 

A particular case in point is the parchment used in the Art. The magician was required to slay a lamb or a young goat <note>original: kid</note> with his own hands, not because there was any symbolic importance attached to the act of destruction—the Grimoires make no such claim—and not because the blood was used (for its use is seldom prescribed), but to ensure that the animal was one from which "virgin parchment" or "virgin vellum" could be prepared.

That is called "virgin," says the *Book of True Black Magic*, which is made from animals that have never mated <note>original: engendered</note>, especially males <gloss>1</gloss>—from which definition it is obvious that going

<page-num>171</page-num>

to a professional tanner might jeopardize the entire instruction. But seeing that every operation undertaken in the Art assumed a religious aspect, the slaughter of the animal became a sacrifice <gloss>1</gloss> and had its accompanying ceremonies, prayers, and invocations, with each ritual possessing its own variations.

The preparation of the virgin parchment is given as follows in the *Grimorium Verum* <note>The "Grimoire of Truth," an 18th-century handbook of black magic</note>: <gloss>2</gloss>—

### CONCERNING THE VICTIM OF THE ART <gloss>3</gloss>

<summary>This section explains that animal sacrifice in grimoires was often a practical necessity for creating "virgin parchment" rather than a purely diabolical act, emphasizing the magician's need for self-reliance in preparing materials.</summary>
<keywords>animal sacrifice, virgin parchment, Key of Solomon, Paracelsus, Grimorium Verum, ritual purity, vellum, Goëtia</keywords>

--- Page 139 ---
<meta>This page continues the instructions for preparing "virgin parchment" from the skin of a sacrificial animal, detailing the ritual skinning, salting, and chemical treatment required to create a holy writing surface for magical seals.</meta>

Take your young goat <note>original: "kid"</note>; place it on a block with the throat turned upward, so that it may be easier for you to cut it; be ready with your knife, and cut the throat at a single stroke, pronouncing the name of the Spirit whom you wish to invoke. For example, say: *I slay you, [Name of animal], in the name and to the honor of [Name of Spirit]. . . .* Take care that two blows are not needed, but let it die at the first; then skin it with the knife, and while skinning it make the following

### *INVOCATION*

> *ADIRAM, DALMAY, LAUDAY, TETRAGRAMMATON <note>The four-letter Hebrew name of God, YHWH</note>, ANERETON <note>A divine name of power</note>, <gloss>4</gloss> and all you holy angels of God, be here and deign to grant*

<page-num>172</page-num>

> *power <note>original: "virtue"</note> unto this skin, that it may be properly preserved <note>original: "conserved"</note>, and that all things written on it may attain their perfection.*

After the skinning, take well-pounded salt; stretch the skin; strew your salt upon it so as to cover the whole surface. But first, let the salt be blessed in the following manner:

### *EXORCISM OF THE SALT*

> *I exorcise you, creature of salt, by the living God, by the God of gods and the Lord of lords, that all illusions may depart from you, and that so you may serve to make the virgin parchment <note>original: "virgin chart"</note>.*

### *BENEDICTION OF THE SALT*

> *God of gods and Lord of lords, Who created all things out of nothing, and specifically designed salt for human health, bless and sanctify this salt, and may I use it so that all things within this circle may receive the required power <note>original: "virtue"</note> for the effect which we desire. Amen.*

When this is ended, place your salted skin in the rays of the sun for the space of one day. Then prepare a vessel of glazed clay and write the symbols <note>original: "characters"</note> of Guland and Surgat <note>Two spirits described in Chapter 3, Section 2 of this book</note> around it with the pen and ink of the Art. Place quicklime <note>calcium oxide, used in tanning to remove hair</note> mixed <note>original: "slaked"</note> with exorcised water into the pot, and while it is liquid, place your skin inside, and leave it there until the hair peels off by itself. When the hair is ready to fall at the touch of a finger, take it from the pot and peel it with a knife of hazel-wood, over which you have pronounced these words: *Most Holy ABOEZRA, be pleased to grant such power <note>original: "virtue"</note> unto this wood that I may cleanse this skin with it, through Your Holy Name AGASON. Amen.*

<page-num>173</page-num>

When this is done and the skin is also cleansed, stretch it upon a board of new wood, and set stones around it of the same length as the sides of the skin. But first, say over them the prayer which follows here:

<vocab>ADIRAM, DALMAY, LAUDAY, TETRAGRAMMATON, ANERETON, Guland, Surgat, ABOEZRA, AGASON, virgin parchment, quicklime, hazel-wood, exorcism, benediction</vocab>

### PRAYER

O ADONAY <note>original: "ADONAY" — A Hebrew title for God meaning "Lord."</note>, most strong and powerful God, grant that these stones may stretch this skin, and do You remove from them all illusion, so that by Your power <note>original: "virtue"</note> they may possess the efficacy which we desire. Amen.

Once this is finished, the skin may be left to dry, but before leaving it, recite the following:

### PRAYER

JE, AGLA, JOD, HOI, HE, EMMANUEL, *be you guardians of this parchment or skin, so that no phantoms may take possession of it.*

When this is finished, leave it in the open air until it is dried, <gloss>1</gloss> taking care that the place is clean and has been sprinkled with holy water <note>original: "asperged"</note> while reciting the following holy words: *In the name of the immortal God, may God sprinkle you and cleanse you from all delusion and from all wickedness; so shall you be whiter than snow. Amen.*

When the skin is dry, remove it from the board, bless it with holy water and incense <note>original: "aspersion and fumigation"</note>, and keep it for future use. It must not be seen by women, especially during their menstrual cycles <note>original: "in their times" — a common superstition in historical grimoires regarding ritual purity</note>, for it will lose its power; furthermore, whoever prepares this parchment must likewise be most pure, clean, and chaste. Let him perform a Mass of the Nativity <note>Christmas Mass</note>, whether it be on the actual feast day or another day, and note that all the ritual tools <note>original: "instruments"</note> must generally be placed upon the Altar. <gloss>1</gloss>

*The Book of True Black Magic* <note>A 17th-century French grimoire based on the "Key of Solomon."</note> reproduces the process of the genuine *Key of Solomon* <note>original: "Clavicle"</note> with further variations: Secure the animal that is to provide the parchment in a secret place, where no one lives or can see it. Then take a new branch <note>original: "virgin rod"</note>; shape it into the form of a knife using the white-handled Knife of the Art, trimming away all the smaller branches neatly. Over this, say: *I conjure you, Rod, by the Creator of the Universe and the King of Angels, Whose name is HELSADAY!* <gloss>2</gloss> <note>original: "HELSADAY" — likely a corruption of the Hebrew "El Shaddai," meaning "God Almighty."</note> *Receive power and efficacy to skin this animal for the making of the parchment, so that all things which I shall write upon it may prosper, by God Almighty, Who lives and reigns for ever and ever. Amen.* While shaping this knife, recite the Psalm *Give the King Your judgments, O God.* <gloss>3</gloss> <note>original: "Deus, judicium tuum Regi da" — the opening of Psalm 72.</note> Then write these words upon the Rod: AGLA, ADONAY, ELOE, <gloss>4</gloss> <note>Names of God in Hebrew: "Thou art strong forever, My Lord, God."</note> *may the work of this wooden Knife be accomplished by you!* Say also over it: CARA, CHERNA, SITO, CIRNA. <gloss>5</gloss> Next, skin the animal with this same wooden knife, saying: ADIRAM, DAHNAY, SADAY, TETRAGRAMMATON, ANERETON, ANERETON, CURETON, <gloss>6</gloss> *Holy Angels of God, be present and give power to this parchment. May it be consecrated by you, and thus may all things which shall be written upon it acquire the power necessary for the attainment of the desired goal. . .*

When you have skinned it, take salt and say over it:

> *God of gods, grant that this salt may be blessed and sanctified, so that it may cleanse the parchment which I am about to prepare with it, and that both may obtain power <note>original: "virtue"</note>, strength, and effect. . . .*

Salt the skin and set it in the sun for fifteen days. Next, take a glazed clay pot and write these symbols <note>original: "characters"</note> around it:

<image-desc>A sequence of eight esoteric magical characters or sigils, including symbols resembling a stylized "f", a "w" with a loop, a "3" with a cross, and other calligraphic glyphs.</image-desc>

Place a large piece of quicklime <note>calcium oxide, used historically to remove hair from hides</note> with some holy water <note>original: "blessed water"</note> in this vessel. When the lime is slaked <note>chemically reacted with the water</note>, soak <note>original: "steep"</note> the skin in it for nine full days. Afterward, it must be taken out and scraped with a wooden knife to remove the hair. Let it dry for eight days in the shade, but when setting it out to dry, sprinkle it with holy water <note>original: "asperge it"</note> and say:

> *In the Name of the Great Eternal God, I sprinkle you. Be cleansed from all sin and wickedness <note>original: "vice and iniquity"</note>!*

[When dry] incense <note>original: "perfume"</note> it with sweet-smelling herbs and wrap it in a silk cloth along with the other ritual tools <note>original: "instruments of the Art"</note>. Take notice that if it is seen by a woman during her menstrual cycle <note>original: "in her times"</note>, it will lose all its power.

But if this kind of parchment is too difficult to prepare, <gloss>1</gloss> take virgin parchment <note>traditionally skin from an unborn or newborn animal</note> made from any animal; exorcise it as described before. Place glowing coals in a new glazed pot, mix high-quality incense <note>original: "good perfumes"</note> with them, and hold your parchment above the vessel to receive the smoke. Before doing anything else, however, write these symbols around the pot <gloss>2</gloss>

<image-desc>A sequence of seven esoteric magical characters or sigils, including a stylized "f", a "U", a "22" with a loop, and other script-like occult symbols.</image-desc>

using the ritual tools. While the incense smoke <note>original: "fumigation"</note> continues, say:

> *Angels of God, be my help, and may my work be accomplished through you! . . .*

When it is finished, say: LAZAY, SALMAY, DALMAY, ANEPATON, CENDRION, ANITOR, ENCHEION <note>These are "barbarous names," or ritual power words common in grimoires, often derived from corrupted Greek or Hebrew</note>, <gloss>3</gloss>

> *Holy Angels of God, be present and give power <note>original: "virtue"</note> to this parchment, so that it may in turn acquire the power of all the symbols <note>original: "characters"</note> with which it shall be inscribed, by the help of the pious and merciful God. . . .* 

Recite the...

Recite the Psalms *Give the King your judgment, O God* <note>original Latin: "Deus, judicium tuum Regi da"; Psalm 72</note> and *Praise the Lord, all you nations* <note>original Latin: "Laudate Dominum omnes gentes"; Psalm 117</note>, adding the following:

### CONJURATION

> *I conjure you, creature of parchment, by all the names of God, that nothing which shall be written upon you may ever be erased from the truth.*

Lastly, sprinkle <note>original: "asperge"</note> it with holy water and place it in the silken cloth, as described above.

The *True Grimoire* <note>original: "Grimorium Verum"</note> indulges the less experienced student by offering a further simplification, as follows: If you are unable to prepare the parchment yourself, buy a new piece and conjure it; sprinkle it and smoke it with incense <note>original: "fumigate"</note> three times. The animal sacrifice, with its delays and unpleasantness, is thus cancelled <note>original: "abrogated"</note>, and the practitioner can proceed at once to the practical mystery of the *Holy Kingdom* <note>original: "Sanctum Regnum"</note>. It would also appear that parchment or vellum itself may be replaced with: (a) the skin of some other animal, which would, however, involve a similar preparation process; or (b) the caul <note>term: a membrane that sometimes covers the head and face of a newborn child</note> of a newborn child. This would naturally possess the required "virgin" character and was simply consecrated according to the following instructions found in *True Black Magic*.

When you have succeeded in obtaining the caul of a newborn child, perfume it with sweet scents and sprinkle it, after which say the following Psalms: *O Lord, hear my prayer* <note>original Latin: "Domine, exaudi orationem meam"; Psalm 102</note> and *O Lord, my God, look upon me* <note>original Latin: "Domine, Deus meus, respice in me"; Psalm 13</note>, with the conjuration attached here: BOSMELETIC, JEYSMY, ETH, HODOMOS, BELUREOS.

> *O Lord, Who did make all things in wisdom; Who did choose Abraham, Your first believer, whose descendants have multiplied like the stars of heaven; Who did appear to Moses, Your servant, surrounded with flaming fire, and did make known to him Your names, which are HEIE, ACER, HEIE <note>original: "I am that I am," from the Hebrew "Ehyeh Asher Ehyeh"</note>—that is to say, I am that I am; Who did also lead Your people dry-shod across the Red Sea; Who gave the Law of Salvation to Moses, Your servant, on Mount Sinai; Who granted Solomon wisdom beyond the measure of men: I humbly adore Your majesty and beg for Your mercy, praying You to consecrate this skin by Your power, O Most Holy ADONAY <note>a Hebrew name for God, meaning "Lord"</note>, Whose reign lasts forever. Amen.*

Sprinkle the caul with the blessed water of the Art, place it in the silken cloth, and when performing the exorcisms, the demands made to the spirits must be written on this skin, which should be treated with the smoke <note>original: "fumigated"</note> of foul-smelling odors.

### § 8. Concerning Aspersion and Cleansing

In the preparation of the instruments, as is already abundantly evident, and in the more important works which follow it, the ceremonies of aspersion <note>term: the act of sprinkling water for ritual purification</note> and fumigation <note>term: the use of incense or smoke to purify an area</note>—that is, of purifying and consecrating—are continually commanded <note>original: "enjoined"</note>. Sprinkling is performed with the *Aspergillus* <note>term: a ritual brush or branch used to sprinkle holy water</note>, which, according to the *Grimorium Verum* <note>original: "True Grimoire"</note>, should be composed of mint, marjoram <note>original: "marjory"</note>, and rosemary, bound together with a thread woven by a virgin girl. It should be made on the day and in the hour of Mercury <note>The "hour of Mercury" refers to planetary hours, a system in which each hour of the day is ruled by a specific celestial body</note>, while the moon is waxing. The pitcher <note>original: "ewer"</note>, according to the rules <note>original: "canons"</note> of procedure established by *True Black Magic*, should be a pot of glazed earthenware <gloss>2</gloss>, filled with fresh spring water in which some salt has been thrown <note>original: "cast"</note>. The following Psalms should be recited over it:— *Lord, do not rebuke me in Your anger* <note>original Latin: "Domine, ne in furore tuo arguas me"; Psalm 6</note>, and *Lord, hear my prayer* <note>original Latin: "Domine, exaudi orationem meam"; Psalm 102 or 143</note>, <gloss>1</gloss> with the addition of this

->PRAYER<-

> *O You most powerful Lord, my God, my rest, and my life! Help me, Most Holy Father! In You I place my hope, You who are the God of Abraham, God of Isaac, God of Jacob, God of the Angels and Archangels, God of the Prophets, and God the Creator of all things. In all humility, and by calling upon Your Holy Name, I pray that You will deign <note>original: "vouchsafe"</note> to bless this water, so that in whatever place it shall be sprinkled, it may sanctify our bodies and our souls, through You, Most Holy ADONAI <note>original: "ADONAY"; a Hebrew title for God meaning "My Lord"</note>, whose reign is without end. Amen.*

The *Aspergillus* recommended by this ritual differs somewhat from the previous one, being made of vervain, periwinkle, sage, mint, valerian, ash, and basil, taking care not to use hyssop <gloss>2</gloss> but rosemary in its place. It should be fitted with a handle of virgin hazel, three palms <note>term: an ancient unit of measurement based on the width of a hand</note> in length, and bound as before. The following symbols <note>original: "characters"</note> should be engraved on one side of the handle:—

This sprinkling brush <note>original: Aspergillus</note> may be used on any occasion with perfect assurance that all phantoms will be expelled from every place sprinkled with water that has been properly exorcised. *In the Name of the Immortal God, may God sprinkle <note>original: asperge</note> you, N. <note>In ritual texts, "N." serves as a placeholder for the name of the person or object being addressed.</note>, and cleanse you from all illusion and from all wickedness, and you shall be whiter than snow.*

When sprinkling, also add: *In the Name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Ghost. Amen.*

### *Concerning Incense and Fumigation*

The *True Grimoire* <note>original: Grimorium Verum</note> prescribes a small pot or incense burner <note>original: cruse or chafing-dish</note> for fumigation <note>term: the use of aromatic smoke to purify an area</note>. It adds, with a decorative wordiness that matches its impossible nature, that it should be filled with freshly lit coals and a new fire, burning brightly. Place aromatics on it and perfume whatever you wish, saying: *Angels of God, be our help, and may our work be fulfilled through you! ZAZAY, SALMAY, DALMAY, ANGERECTON, LEDRION, AMISOR, EUCHEY, OR! <note>These are "barbarous names of power," phonetic strings used in magic to command spiritual forces.</note> Great Angels! And do You also, O ADONAY <note>original: Hebrew for "Lord"</note>, be present and grant this thing such power that this created thing <note>original: creature; in this context, it refers to the material substance of the incense</note> may receive a form, so that our work may be accomplished! In the Name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Ghost. Amen. . . . Recite the Seven Psalms which follow "Give Your judgment to the King" <note>original: Judicium tuum Regi da; Psalm 72</note> and "Praise the Lord, all you nations" <note>original: Laudate Dominum omnes gentes; Psalm 117</note>.* 1

The perfumes consist of aloeswood <note>a fragrant, resinous wood</note> and incense for most occasions, and mace <note>a spice derived from the outer shell of nutmeg</note> for the fumigation of the ritual circle. *The Book of True Black Magic* omits mace but substitutes benzoin <note>a balsamic resin</note> and storax <note>a fragrant resin from the Liquidambar tree</note>, as well as any others you choose, without making a specific distinction for the circle. The following Prayers, Exorcisms, and Blessings <note>original: Benedictions</note>, to be said over the aromatic perfumes, appear in both Rituals. The first and third are common to both and taken from the *Key of Solomon*; the second, also originating from that same source, is not found in the *True Grimoire*.

### *FIRST EXORCISM*

*O God of Abraham, God of Isaac, and God of Jacob, grant Your blessing and sanctify these created things <note>original: creatures</note>, of whatever kind they may be, so that they may obtain*

### SECOND EXORCISM

> *I exorcise you, O impure spirit, who are the phantom of the enemy. In the name of God Almighty, come out from this substance <note>original: "kind," referring to the specific type of incense</note>, with all your falsehood and wickedness, so that it may become sanctified and exorcised in the name of God Almighty. May all virtues inspire those who partake of this fragrance; may the Spirit of God descend, and the power of the Lord manifest, in the burning of this material <note>original: "species"</note>; and may no phantom dare to approach it. Through the ineffable name of God Almighty. Amen.*

### THIRD EXORCISM

> *Graciously allow, O Lord, the sanctification of this creature <note>term: in ritual magic, "creature" refers to any created element or object—such as water, salt, or incense—that is being consecrated for a spiritual purpose</note>, so that it may become a powerful remedy for the human race and the salvation of our souls and bodies. Through the invocation of Your Most Holy Name, may all creatures who inhale the smoke of this substance receive health of body and soul. Through that Lord who has created the ages of ages <note>original: "saecula saeculorum," a Latin liturgical phrase meaning "forever and ever"</note>. So be it.*

Once this is done, sprinkle the perfumes with exorcised water, and set them aside in the silken cloth to be used as directed. When you wish to fumigate <note>term: the ritual use of aromatic smoke to purify an area or attract spirits</note> anything, take new charcoal that has not been previously lit. Set it on fire, and while it is still black <note>meaning: just beginning to catch fire before turning gray with ash</note>, exorcise it by saying:

### EXORCISM OF THE LIGHTED COAL

> *I exorcise you, O creature of fire, by Him who has made all things! Drive out from yourself all phantoms, so that they may in no way harm or trouble us in our work, by the invocation of the Most High Creator. Amen. O Savior, Almighty and merciful, bless Your creature of this kind, so that no harm may come to him who shall make use of it. Amen.*

In concluding this portion of its ceremony, the *Book of True Black Magic* <note>original: "Le Livre de la Véritable Noire Magie," an 18th-century French grimoire adapted from the Key of Solomon</note> states that there are occasions when perfumes of a stinking or foul-smelling <note>original: "malodorous"</note> kind must be used. As the first step of their preparation, the operator must say over them: Adonai, Lazay, Delmay, Amay, Saday, Eloy <gloss>1</gloss> <note>These are "barbarous names of power," often derived from corrupted Hebrew names of God, used to command spiritual forces.</note>, by the invocation, O Savior, of Your Most Holy Name, grant us, through this substance, the help of Your grace, and may it assist us in all things which we need to fulfill; may all malice pass out from here, and may it be blessed and sanctified in Your most powerful Name. Amen.

Sprinkle <note>original: "Asperge"</note> the foul-smelling perfume with exorcised water, place it in the silken cloth, and use it as needed—but always in the name of the Most Holy Adonai. By doing so, you shall obtain your desire, says the "Manuscript discovered at Jerusalem in the Sepulchre of Solomon" <gloss>2</gloss> <note>This refers to the legendary origin story common to many grimoires, claiming the texts were found in the tomb of King Solomon to grant them ancient authority.</note>.

## § 9. Concerning the Time of Operation

Once the "sacrifice of the Art" <note>term: a ritual animal sacrifice, or a symbolic substitute, required to prepare the tools of magic</note> or its substitute has been completed, the practitioner possesses all the materials required in theory <note>original: "ex hypothesi"</note> for performing Goëtic <note>term: magic involving the invocation of spirits or demons, often considered "low" or black magic</note> works as described in the *Book of True Black Magic* or the *Grimorium Verum*. 

However, just as the first of these rituals commands that the practitioner's preparation must follow certain planetary influences, it also regulates the timing of the actual operation or its completion. The instructions continue to follow the authority of the Clavicle <note>term: another name for the *Key of Solomon* (from the Latin "Clavicula," meaning "Little Key")</note>, though they do so in a confused manner. It is useful to reproduce them here in a brief summary, even though the fragmented nature of the manual attributed to Iroë-Grego <note>term: a pseudonym for the supposed author of certain versions of "The True Black Magic"</note> leaves these rules without much practical value, especially since other creators of Grimoires ignore them entirely:

<summary>This page concludes the technical footnotes regarding ritual tools and materials, then introduces the rules for timing magical operations according to planetary influences as dictated by various Grimoires.</summary>
<keywords>Divine Names, Key of Solomon, Vulgate, perfumes, Lycanthropy, Goëtic magic, planetary influences, Iroë-Grego, ritual timing</keywords>

--- Page 148 ---
<meta>This page continues the specific instructions for timing magical operations based on planetary days and hours, following the general introduction to astrological considerations in ritual magic.</meta>

An experiment for speaking with spirits or summoning them should be performed on the day and in the hour of Mercury. This hour may be the first or the eighth, but the fifteenth or twenty-second hour of the same night will be even better, for spirits appear more easily in the silence of the dark hours. A certain atmospheric condition is also required for the experiment of calling spirits, and a dark, uninhabited place appropriate for such a craft is even more necessary; only in such a setting will the ritual be successfully completed and brought forth <note>original: "educed"</note>. 

But if the experiment concerns a theft, <gloss>1</gloss> <note>In traditional magic, these rituals were often intended to identify a thief or recover stolen property.</note> it should be performed in the hour of the Moon and on her day—if possible, during the waxing Moon—from the first to the eighth hour, or, failing this, at ten o'clock at night. It is better, however, to perform this during the day, because the light has a stronger connection to the intention—that is, the revealing of hidden things. 

The ritual for invisibility <gloss>1</gloss> should be performed when the Moon is in the zodiac sign of Pisces. Experiments of love, favor, and grace <gloss>2</gloss> should be completed on the day and in the hour of the Sun or Venus, from the first to the eighth hour, with the Moon in Pisces. 

Works of destruction, hatred, and ruin <gloss>3</gloss> should be performed on the day and in the hour of Saturn, from the first or eighth hour until the fifteenth or twenty-second hour of the night; performed then, their success is certain. Humorous and entertaining <note>original: "burlesque and amusing"</note> experiments are performed on the day of Venus, in the first and the eighth hour, or in the fifteenth and twenty-second hour of the night. Extraordinary rituals should be performed during the corresponding hours on the day of Jupiter. <gloss>4</gloss>

# CHAPTER III
## Concerning the Descending Hierarchy
### § 1. The Names and Offices of Evil Spirits

In all ceremonial matters, the inspiration for these magic books (grimoires) must be sought in the actual *Key of Solomon* <note>original: "Key of Solomon"; a foundational 14th- or 15th-century book of ceremonial magic.</note>. However, for the names and duties of demons, one must look to the *Lesser Key*. The hierarchy of spirits according to the *Lemegeton* <note>original: "Lemegeton"; another name for the Lesser Key of Solomon.</note>, along with the rituals for summoning them, will be provided in the fourth chapter. There is no particular reason to separate those names from their practical instructions. Therefore, I will deal here with simpler, though more recent, summaries to prevent these curious topics from becoming confused.

The *Book of True Black Magic*, much like its model the original *Clavicle* <note>original: "Clavicle"; from the Latin 'Clavicula', meaning a small key.</note>, provides no explanation of the nature of the spirits it claims to control. As we have already seen, it ends abruptly just at the point where the summoning formulas should begin. Since this "Secret of Secrets"—the term grimoires use for the methods of summoning and dismissing spirits—is omitted, we can set this book aside until we reach the minor rituals of Ceremonial Magic in the eighth chapter. These rituals are mostly "venereal" <note>In this context, rituals concerning love, sex, and attraction.</note> or focus on creating illusions.

The *Grimoire of Honorius* mentions four kings by name who are associated with the cardinal directions (North, South, East, and West), as well as seven lesser spirits to be summoned on specific days of the week. However, as we will see later, it does not provide a clear description of the Descending Hierarchy <note>The organized ranking of demons from highest to lowest power.</note>. These gaps are filled by the *Grimorium Verum* and the *Grand Grimoire*.

<summary>Waite explains that while ceremonial instructions come from the Key of Solomon, the names and ranks of demons are found in the Lesser Key and other grimoires like the Grimorium Verum.</summary>
<keywords>Descending Hierarchy, Grimoires, Key of Solomon, Lemegeton, Book of True Black Magic, Grimoire of Honorius, Grimorium Verum, Grand Grimoire, Demonology</keywords>

--- Page 152 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the hierarchies and summoning methods found in various grimoires, specifically comparing the organizational structures of the "Grimorium Verum" and the "Grand Grimoire."</meta>

As for the spirits, the first source says that some are superior and others are inferior. The three superior spirits are Lucifer, Beelzebuth, and Astaroth. According to the second source, Lucifer is the Emperor, Beelzebuth is the Prince, and Astaroth is the Grand Duke. They must be summoned using the sigils <note>original: "characters"; these are symbolic signs or occult marks used in magic</note> which they themselves have revealed. These sigils must be written with the blood of the practitioner <note>original: "operator"</note> or with the blood of a sea turtle <note>original: "sea-tortoise"</note>. If this is not possible, they may be engraved on an emerald or a ruby, for both of these stones have a great affinity with spirits—especially those of the "solar race," who are wiser, better, and friendlier than the rest. 1

The sigil must be worn on the person. In the case of a man, it should be placed in the right pocket; however, a woman must carry it between her breasts, toward the left side, like a reliquary <note>a container used to hold sacred relics</note>. It should be engraved or written on the day and in the hour of Mars <note>an astrological timing used to capture the planet's influence</note> for both sexes, and apparently for each of the three spirits.

The lord commands the servants, the grimoire continues, and these three spirits have power over all things. But the practitioner is advised that he must obey those who obey him, because these exalted and powerful spirits serve only their confidants and close friends. He is further sternly warned to remain on his guard so that no spirit seizes him unexpectedly. Each of the rulers has two chief officers who announce to their subjects everything their lord has commanded throughout the world and decree everything that is to be done. The subordinates of Lucifer are *Put Satanachia* and *Agaliarept*; they inhabit Europe and Asia. Those of Beelzebuth are *Tarchimache* and *Fleurety*; they dwell in Africa. Those of Astaroth are *Sargatanas* and *Nebiros*, whose refuge is America; this suggests that this specific kind of magic was developed after the time of Columbus <note>original: "post-Columbian"</note>.

The *Grand Grimoire* does not divide the six subordinate spirits among three rulers, but instead lists them and their titles <note>original: "dignities"</note> somewhat differently, as follows: 2—

**LUCIFUGE ROFOCALE** <note>original name likely derived from the Latin "lux" (light) and "fugere" (to flee), meaning "he who flees the light"</note> — Prime Minister.

**SATANACHIA** — Commander-in-Chief.

**AGALIAREPT** — Another Commander.

**FLEURETY** — Lieutenant-General.

**SARGATANAS** — Brigadier-Major.

**NEBIROS** — Field-Marshal and Inspector-General.

<summary>This page details the hierarchy of primary spirits (Lucifer, Beelzebuth, Astaroth) and their subordinates in traditional grimoires, including specific ritual instructions for creating and wearing their sigils. It also highlights the military-style organization of the spirit world described in the Grand Grimoire.</summary>
<keywords>Lucifer, Beelzebuth, Astaroth, Lucifuge Rofocale, sigils, talismans, Grand Grimoire, spirit hierarchy, occult ritual, Mars, demonology</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the catalog of infernal spirits and their specific powers, shifting from the three primary rulers to their chief officers and subordinates as described in the *Grand Grimoire* and the *True Grimoire*.</meta>

It will be seen that the principalities of Hell <note>original: "Infernus"</note> are represented primarily as a standing army, yet their involvement in earthly matters is not military in nature. **LUCIFUGE ROFOCALE** has the control—granted to him by **Lucifer**—over all the wealth and treasures of the world. His subordinates are **Baal**, <gloss>1</gloss> *Agares* <gloss>2</gloss> and **Marbas**. <gloss>3</gloss> The grand **SATANACHIA** has the power to compel all wives and young women to submit to his wishes, and to do with them as he wills. His subordinates are *Pruslas, Aamon* <gloss>4</gloss> and *Barbatos.* <gloss>5</gloss> **AGALIAREPT** has the faculty of discovering hidden secrets in all the courts and council-chambers of the world; he also unveils the most profound mysteries. He commands the Second Legion of Spirits, and his subordinates are *Buer*, <gloss>6</gloss> *Gusoyn* <gloss>7</gloss> and *Botis.* <gloss>8</gloss> **FLEURETY** has the power to carry out any labor during the night and to cause hailstones

in any required place. He controls a massive army of spirits and has *Bathsin* <gloss>1</gloss> (or *Bathim*), *Pursan*, and *Eligor* <gloss>2</gloss> as his subordinates. **SARGATANAS** has the power to make anyone invisible, to transport them anywhere, to open all locks, to reveal whatever is happening in private houses, and to teach all the skills of shepherds. He commands several Brigades of Spirits and has *Zoray, Valefar*, and *Faraii* as his immediate inferiors. **NEBIROS** has the power to harm whomever he chooses; he discovers the Hand of Glory <note>a charm made from the dried and pickled hand of a man who has been hanged, used by burglars to render people motionless</note> and reveals every property <note>original: "virtue"</note> of metals, minerals, and vegetables, as well as all animals, both pure and impure. He possesses the art of predicting the future, being one of the greatest necromancers in all the Infernal Hierarchies; he travels everywhere and inspects the legions of the damned. His immediate subordinates are *Ayperos, Naberrs*, and *Glassyalabolas.*

It is added that there are millions of other spirits in subjection to those who have been mentioned, but listing them serves no purpose, as they are required only when it pleases the superiors to employ them in their own place; for the superiors make use of all the lower intelligences like workmen or slaves.

The *True Grimoire* <note>original: "Grimorium Verum"</note>, however, mentions seventeen of the most important subordinate spirits who seem to correspond with the ministers of the six great leaders mentioned above, but there is little agreement among the authors of magical rituals, whether they are "white" or "black" magic.

who makes a pact with him. He can grant great wealth, for he is well-loved by **Lucifer**. He brings money from a distance. Obey him, and he will obey you.

## § 2. Concerning the Forms of Infernal Spirits in their Manifestations

Considering the nature and history of the spirits <note>original: "Intelligences"</note> with which Black Magic claims to deal, it is very important that the practitioner should know what kind of apparitions to expect when—despite the skepticism of Horatio <note>A reference to Shakespeare's *Hamlet*: "There are more things in heaven and earth, Horatio, / Than are dreamt of in your philosophy."</note>—the spirits from the "vasty deep" <note>A reference to Shakespeare's *Henry IV, Part 1*: "I can call spirits from the vasty deep."</note> actually respond to summoning <note>original: "conjuration"</note>. Most grimoires <term>grimoire: a textbook of magic, typically providing instructions on how to summon spirits or perform spells</term> imply by their silence that they appear in human form—possibly in "a neat overcoat <note>original: "surcoat"</note> and snow-white linen," like the *Adonay* of the sorcerer in the *Magical Experiences* <note>original: "Thaumaturgic Experiences"</note> of Éliphas Lévi. In the "Composite Rituals" <note>Rituals that combine elements from various different magical traditions</note>, this is always a cause for concern, and a peaceful appearance is earnestly requested.

According to the *Grimorium Verum* <note>original: "The True Grimoire," an 18th-century book of magic</note>, the spirits do not always appear in the same forms, and we have seen some surprising examples of their changes in earlier texts. Because they are separate from all physical matter, they must necessarily borrow a body in order to appear before us, <gloss>1</gloss> and they then take on whatever shape and figure seems best to them. "Beware, however, that they do not frighten you"—is another significant warning. Lucifer appears in the form and figure of a handsome <note>original: "comely"</note> boy; when angry, he is shown with a red <note>original: "ruddy"</note> face, but there is nothing monstrous about his shape. Beelzebuth <note>Commonly known as Beelzebub</note> occasionally appears in monstrous forms, such as the figure of a deformed calf or a goat with a long tail; at the same time, he most frequently appears in the form of a giant fly. <gloss>2</gloss> When angry, he spits out floods of water and howls like a wolf. Astaroth appears in a black and white color, usually in a human form, but occasionally in the likeness of a donkey <note>original: "ass"</note>.

<summary>This page discusses how infernal spirits like Lucifer, Beelzebub, and Astaroth manifest to practitioners, noting that while they lack physical bodies, they take on various human or animal forms during rituals.</summary>
<keywords>Lemegeton, Grimorium Verum, Lucifer, Beelzebub, Astaroth, demonic manifestations, grimoire, Eliphas Levi, spirit forms</keywords>

--- Page 160 ---
<meta>This page transitions from discussing the visual forms spirits take during manifestations to introducing the specific spirits found in the "Lesser Key of Solomon." It explores the legendary background of the seventy-two spirits imprisoned by King Solomon.</meta>

The subject of infernal manifestations is, however, fully treated in the next chapter. The descriptions in the present section are merely the later variations.

### Footnotes

194:1 A portion of this statement is derived from the *Key of Solomon*; but whereas it is stated clearly in the Grimoire <term>grimoire: a textbook of magic, typically providing instructions on how to summon spirits</term>, it is incomplete and illogical in the original. It represents the alternative side of the Kabbalistic <note>original: "Kabalistic"</note> principle that a spirit "clothes" itself in a form to descend to the physical world and "undresses" to return upward. When the abyss releases these forms, its capacity for manifestation is ever-changing <note>original: "Protean"</note>, but its most frequent tendency is toward the monstrous. These forms then disrobe so that the abyss can open and receive them when they return to their origin.

194:2 A giant, a serpent, or a woman are also other ways he may manifest, according to the opinions of demonologists.

# CHAPTER IV

## The Mysteries of Goetic Theurgy according to the Lesser Key of Solomon the King
<note>Goetic Theurgy refers to "Goetia," the practice of invoking spirits or demons, derived from the Greek word for "sorcery."</note>

### §1 Concerning the Spirits of the Brass Vessel, otherwise called the False Monarchy of Demons

The Goetic Art of Solomon <gloss>1</gloss> gives instructions for the summoning <note>original: "evocation"</note> of the seventy-two spirits whom the King of Israel, according to a well-known legend, shut up in a brass vessel and cast into a deep lake. When the vessel was discovered by the Babylonians, they supposed it contained a great treasure and accordingly broke it open. The spirits who were thus set free, together with their armies <note>original: "legions"</note>, returned to their former places, with the exception of Belial. He entered into a certain idol <note>original: "image"</note> and gave prophecies <note>original: "oracles"</note> to the people of that country in return for sacrifices and divine honors; because of this, there is questionable Kabbalistic authority for concluding that demons were satisfied and sustained by such worship. Their names and duties <note>original: "offices"</note> are as follows:

---

### Footnotes

195:1 Although it is a work of considerable length, this *Lesser Key*—as far as it concerns our investigation—can be reduced to a manageable size by omitting the tedious and excessive <note>original: "egregious"</note> details regarding the number of spirits who are subject to a particular Lord, Prince, or Emperor and who are likely to appear in his company. It should be understood in a general way that there are hundreds, thousands, and millions of them, depending on the rank...

...of the spiritual leader <note>original: "hierarch"</note>; they must have been a source of considerable inconvenience, and even of dismay, to the practitioner <note>original: "operator"</note>!

<image-desc>A magical sigil labeled as the Seal of Baal. It consists of a central oval with a cross inside, overlaying two crossed poles or stylized axes, with a small circle and loop at the bottom.</image-desc>

->THE SEAL OF BAAL<-

I. **BAAL**, a king ruling in the East, who grants invisibility and wisdom. He appears with a human head, or with that of a toad or cat, 1 but sometimes with all three at once. He speaks with a raspy <note>original: "hoarse"</note> voice. 2

<image-desc>Three magical seals arranged in a horizontal row. The first on the left is a cup-like or shield-like form with a cross in the center and ornate scrollwork on the sides. The second, in the middle, is a stylized humanoid figure with a crescent at the top and two interlocking circles at the base. The third, on the right, is a tall vertical rectangle topped with a crescent, containing a box at the bottom with the name "Vassago" written in decorative Gothic script.</image-desc>

->THE SEAL OF AGARES<-
->THE SEAL OF VASSAGO<-
->THE SEAL OF VASSAGO USED IN WHITE MAGIC<-

II. **AGARES**, a duke ruling in the East, who appears in the form of a handsome old man, riding at a relaxed pace <note>original: "ambling"</note> upon a crocodile and carrying a hawk <note>original: "goshawk"</note> on his wrist. He makes those who run stand still and brings back those who have fled. He teaches all languages, destroys both spiritual and earthly honors <note>original: "temporal dignities"</note>, and causes earthquakes. 3 He belongs to the Order of the Virtues <note>The Virtues are one of the nine choirs of angels in traditional theology; here, Agares is a fallen angel of that rank</note>. 4

III. **VASSAGO**, a mighty prince with a nature similar to Agares, who reveals things of the past, present, and future, and discovers what has been lost or hidden. He is considered good by nature. 1

IV. **GAMYGYN**, a great marquis, appearing in the form of a small horse or donkey <note>original: "ass"</note>, but afterwards in human shape. He speaks with a raspy <note>original: "hoarsely"</note> voice, teaching the liberal sciences <note>the traditional academic subjects: grammar, logic, rhetoric, arithmetic, geometry, music, and astronomy</note> and providing news of souls who have died in sin. <gloss>2</gloss>

V. **MARBAS**, <gloss>3</gloss> a president <note>a high-ranking title within the infernal hierarchy</note>, who appears as a mighty lion and then in human shape. <gloss>4</gloss> He answers truthfully concerning all things hidden or secret, causes and cures diseases, imparts skill in the mechanical arts <note>original: "mechanics"</note>, and changes men into various shapes. <gloss>5</gloss>

VI. **VALEFOR**, <gloss>6</gloss> a powerful duke, appearing as a many-headed lion. <gloss>7</gloss> He leads those with whom he is familiar into theft.

<image-desc>The Seal of Amon: A vertical central stem ending in a cross at the bottom. The top portion consists of four rounded, petal-like loops arranged in a cluster, each containing a small circle. Curved line flourishes extend from the sides and bottom of the cluster.</image-desc>
->THE SEAL OF AMON<-

VII. **AMON**, a strong and powerful marquis, who appears like a wolf with a serpent's head <gloss>1</gloss> and breathing <note>original: "vomiting"</note> flame. When so ordered, he assumes a human shape, but with the teeth of a dog. <gloss>2</gloss> He perceives <note>original: "discerns"</note> the past and future, obtains love, and reconciles friends and foes.

<meta>This page continues the catalog of the seventy-two spirits of the Goetia, detailing the eighth and ninth entities: Barbatos and Paimon. VIII. **BARBATOS**, a great count and duke, who appears when the sun is in Sagittarius with four noble kings and three companies of troops. He provides instruction in all the sciences, reveals treasures hidden by magic <note>original: "enchantment"</note>, knows the past and future, reconciles friends and those in power, and belongs to the Order of Virtues <note>one of the nine traditional hierarchies, or "choirs," of angels</note>. <gloss>3</gloss>

<image-desc>Line drawing of the Seal of Paimon, featuring a horizontal bar with several vertical lines and small circles at the intersections and ends, forming a stylized glyph within a rectangular framework.</image-desc>

->THE SEAL OF PAIMON<-

IX. **PAIMON**, a great king, very obedient to Lucifer. <gloss>4</gloss> He appears as a crowned man seated on a dromedary <note>a one-humped camel</note>, preceded by all kinds of musicians. He speaks with a roaring voice, teaches all arts, sciences, and secrets, grants and confirms high-ranking titles <note>original: "dignities"</note>, makes others subject to the magician's will, and provides helpful familiars <note>spirit assistants</note>. He is found toward the Northwest and belongs to the Order of Dominions <note>another of the celestial hierarchies</note>. <gloss>5</gloss>

### Footnotes

196:1 The cat is the traditional domestic favorite, not only of those who practice devil-worship <note>original: "diabolise"</note>, but of the diabolical world itself. This shows that, despite everything, there is a strong link to humanity within the ranks of Lucifer. The reader will recall that the cat lands on its feet not by natural luck, but by the special permission of Muhammad, and that the Prophet's favorite cat is in Paradise, likely on the best terms with the dog of the Seven Sleepers <note>an ancient legend of a group of youths and their dog who hid in a cave to escape persecution and slept for centuries</note>.

196:2 Another manuscript <note>original: "codex"</note> says that he appears with three heads: the first like a toad, the second like a man, and the third like a cat.

196:3 According to the *Infernal Vocabulary* <note>original: "Vocabulaire Infernal"</note>, the special role of **Agares** is to drive away the enemies of those he protects.

196:4 That is, he belonged to this choir during the original state <note>the time before the fall</note> of the Fallen Angels.

197:1 This may explain his summoning, especially in ceremonial crystal gazing <note>original: "Crystallomancy"</note>, by the supposed experts <note>original: "putative adepts"</note> of White Magic—as the most doubtful of all arts is called by those who look down on it.

197:2 According to Johann Weyer <note>original: "Wierus," a 16th-century physician and occultist</note>, he summons into the presence of the magician <note>original: "exorcist"</note> the souls of drowned men and those held in Purgatory, called magically *Cartagra*—which means "the affliction of souls." They take on an ethereal body, are visible to the eye, and answer questions. Another version says that **Gamygyn** stays with the magician until he has fulfilled his requests.

197:3 Or Barbas.

XII. **SYTRY**, a great prince, who appears with a leopard's head and the wings of a griffin, but takes on a beautiful human form at the magician's command. He brings about love between the sexes and causes women to show themselves naked. <gloss>3</gloss>

He must be received politely and with honor <note>original: "homage"</note>, but a silver ring must be worn on the middle finger of the left hand, which must be held against the face <note>This was traditionally done to protect the magician from the spirit's breath or presence.</note>. 

He brings about love between man and woman and belongs to the Order of the Powers <note>The Powers (or Potestates) are the sixth rank of angels in the traditional celestial hierarchy.</note>. <gloss>1</gloss>

He reveals hidden things, causes war, organizes <note>original: "marshals"</note> armies, and sparks love and lust. <gloss>2</gloss>

XIX. **SALEOS**, <note>3</note> a great duke, who appears as a gallant <note>original: "brave"</note> soldier riding on a crocodile and wearing a duke's crown. He promotes love between the sexes.

# XX. PURSON, <gloss>4</gloss> a great king, who appears like a man with the head of a lion, carrying a viper in his hand and riding on a bear, preceded by many trumpeters. He conceals and discovers treasure, reveals the past, present, and future, gives true answers regarding things human and divine, and provides good familiar spirits. <gloss>5</gloss>

XXIX. ASTAROTH, a great and powerful duke, appears like a beautiful angel <sub><u>1</u></sub> riding on an infernal dragon and carrying a viper in his right hand. good reputation and the knowledge of languages <note>original: "tongues"</note>, and causes men to be loved by their enemies just as much as by their friends.

204:2 But he claims that he himself was not included in the rebellion and fall of the angels <note>original: "exempt from their lapse"</note> (according to Johann Weyer).

<image-desc>The Seal of Foras: A linear sigil consisting of a horizontal bar intersected by four vertical lines of varying heights, some topped with crosses or small circles. Below the main line is a small square structure resembling a simplified building or pedestal.</image-desc>

->THE SEAL OF FORAS<-

XXXI. FORAS, <note>1</note> a great president, who appears in the form of a strong man. He teaches the hidden powers <note>original: "virtues"</note> of all herbs and precious stones, as well as logic and ethics. He can make men invisible, grants wit, wisdom, and eloquence, discovers hidden treasures, and restores lost items.

<image-desc>The Seal of Asmoday: A complex, curvilinear sigil featuring multiple loops, swirls, and intersecting lines. It includes a crescent shape on the left, several small circles at various points, and a small cross on the upper right side.</image-desc>

->THE SEAL OF ASMODAY<-

XXXII. ASMODAY, <note>2</note> a strong and powerful king, appears with three heads: the first like a bull, the second like a man, and the third like a ram. He has a serpent's tail, the webbed feet of a goose, and he vomits fire. He rides an infernal dragon, carries a lance and a banner <note>original: "pennon"</note>, and is the chief of the power of *Amaymon*. He must be summoned <note>original: "invoked"</note> while the magician is bareheaded, <note>3</note> otherwise the spirit will practice deception. He provides the "ring of virtues," teaches arithmetic, geomancy <note>original: "geomancy"; a method of divination using patterns in the earth or sand</note>, and all handicrafts. He answers all questions, makes men invisible, points out the locations of hidden treasures, and guards them if they are within the realm of *Amaymon*.

XXXIII. GAAP, <note>4</note> <note>Note: The original text repeats the number XXXII, which is likely a typographical error for XXXIII.</note> a great president and prince, appears when the sun is in the southern zodiac signs. He comes in human form, preceded by four powerful kings. He teaches philosophy

LII. **ALLOCEN**, <gloss>2</gloss> a powerful duke, appears in the form of a soldier mounted on a large horse. His face resembles that of a lion and is exceedingly red; his eyes glow like fire, and his speech is hoarse and loud. He teaches astronomy and the <term>liberal sciences</term> <note>the traditional academic subjects: grammar, logic, rhetoric, arithmetic, geometry, music, and astronomy</note>, and provides a good <term>familiar</term> <note>a minor spirit servant</note>.

LIII. **CAIM**, a great president, appears in the form of a thrush <note>a type of songbird</note>, <gloss>3</gloss> but afterwards takes the shape of a man carrying a sharp sword. He seems—as it is fancifully said—to give his answers from within burning ashes. He is a keen debater; he grants men the ability to understand the songs of birds, the lowing of cattle, the barking of dogs, and the voice of rushing waters. He provides true answers concerning the future and once belonged to the Order of Angels.

LIV. **MURMUR**, a great duke and earl, appears in the form of a soldier riding upon a <term>griffin</term> <note>a mythical creature with the body of a lion and the head and wings of an eagle</note> and wearing a duke's crown upon his head. He is preceded by two ministers blowing trumpets. He teaches philosophy perfectly and forces the souls of the dead to appear and answer his questions. He formerly belonged partly to the Order of Thrones and partly to the Order of Angels.

LVI. **GOMORY**, a powerful duke, appears as a beautiful woman wearing a ducal crown <gloss>1</gloss> <note>Other versions of the text specify the crown is tied around her waist</note>. He <note>The text uses masculine pronouns despite the spirit's female appearance</note> reveals the past, present, and future, as well as the whereabouts of hidden treasures; he procures the love of women, especially young girls.

LVII. **OSE**, a great president, appears at first like a leopard and then in human shape. He can change men into any shape that the exorcist may desire, and the person who is changed will not even be aware of it. <gloss>1</gloss> <note>The victim believes they are still in their original form, or is completely lost in the delusion of the new shape</note>.

LVIII. **AMY**, a great president, appears first as a great flaming fire and then in human form. He provides perfect knowledge of astrology and the <term>liberal sciences</term> <note>the seven traditional branches of knowledge: grammar, logic, rhetoric, arithmetic, geometry, music, and astronomy</note>, grants excellent <term>familiars</term> <note>spirit assistants</note>, and can reveal <note>original: "betray"</note> treasures that are guarded by spirits. <gloss>2</gloss> <note>See footnote 214:2 for Amy’s history</note>

LIX. **ORIAS**, a great marquis, appears in the form of a lion riding a strong horse; he has a serpent's tail and holds two enormous, hissing snakes in his right hand. He teaches the virtues of the planets and their <term>mansions</term> <note>the specific positions of the planets in the zodiac used in astrology</note>; he transforms men and bestows <term>dignities</term> and <term>prelacies</term> <note>high-ranking titles and offices in the church or state</note>, as well as official confirmations and the favor of both friends and enemies.

LX. **VAPULA**, a powerful duke, appears in the form of a lion with the wings of a griffin. He grants skill not only in manual professions but also in philosophy and the sciences found in books—presumably, as the author suggests, to distinguish these from the "secret" or occult sciences.

<image-desc>The Seal of Zagan: A linear occult sigil featuring a central vertical axis with a horizontal crossbar. To the left is a star-like structure composed of crossed lines with small circles at six of the eight points. To the right is a large semi-circular, bow-like shape.</image-desc>

LXI. **ZAGAN**, a great king and president, appears at first in the form of a bull with griffin's wings, but later takes human shape. ->THE SEAL OF FLAUROS<-

LXIV. **FLAUROS**, a great duke, appears at first as a terrifying leopard, but at the command of the exorcist, he takes the form of a man with fiery eyes and a fearsome expression. He gives true answers regarding things past, present, and future, but unless he is commanded to stay within the <term>triangle</term> <note>the "Triangle of Art," a protective space where the spirit is constrained during a ritual to prevent it from deceiving the magician</note>, he will deceive the exorcist. He speaks willingly about theology <note>original: "divinity"</note>...

72. **ANDROMALIUS**, a great duke and earl, appears in the form of a man holding a serpent in his hand. He returns stolen goods, reveals the thief, discovers all wickedness and deceitful <note>original: "underhand"</note> dealing, as well as hidden treasures.

Such are the primary mysteries of *Goëtia* <note>original: "Goëtia"; a term derived from Greek meaning "sorcery" or "witchcraft," typically referring to the invocation of demons.</note> according to the tradition of the *Lemegeton* <note>The "Lesser Key of Solomon," a 17th-century grimoire.</note>; such is the work which has been described by supporters of White Magic as being distinct from the *Key of Solomon*, and as being highly important and valuable in its own field. <gloss>1</gloss> Viewed in the light of these seventy-two <note>original: "two-and-seventy"</note> methods of accomplishing all manner of abominations, it would seem that the three "analogical realms of occult philosophy" <note>A reference to the occult theories of Eliphas Lévi, who organized the universe into three levels or "worlds."</note> are simply the three kingdoms which we have known by other names since our childhood—as "the world, the flesh, and the devil."

After listing them, it will be unnecessary to argue any longer regarding the distinction between Black and White Magic as it appears in one of the oldest and best of the Rituals. There is no longer any need to inquire whether the practitioner <note>original: "operator"</note> is saved by his intention, for we are now well-acquainted with the nature of the intentions which govern the procedures in one of the most reputable memorials of "White Magic" still in existence <note>original: "extant"</note>. The summoning <note>original: "evocation"</note> of Lucifer and Astaroth, whatever the excuse, must be—

<summary>Waite completes the list of the seventy-two spirits of the Goetia with Andromalius and begins a sharp critique of the Lemegeton, arguing that its contents are inherently "Black Magic" despite historical claims to the contrary.</summary>
<keywords>Andromalius, Dantalian, Goetia, Lemegeton, White Magic, Black Magic, Occult Philosophy, Lucifer, Astaroth</keywords>

## § 2. Concerning the Rite of Conjuration, from the Lemegeton

When communicating with the seventy-two spirits, the age of the Moon should be observed. According to the authority of Solomon, all days are favorable <note>original: "profitable"</note> except for the second, fourth, sixth, ninth, tenth, twelfth, or fourteenth day of the lunar cycle <note>original: "of that luminary"</note>.

<summary>Waite dismisses the moral distinction between different types of grimoires and introduces the "Rite of Conjuration" from the Lemegeton, focusing on the lunar timing required for success.</summary>
<keywords>Triangle of Solomon, Michael, Tetragrammaton, Anexhexeton, Primeumaton, Satanism, Lemegeton, Grimoire of Honorius, Mathers, Lunar Timing, Conjuration</keywords>

--- Page 186 ---
<meta>This page continues the technical instructions for the Goetia (the first book of the Lesser Key of Solomon), specifically detailing the astrological and material requirements for creating the seals of the seventy-two spirits and the specific times they may be summoned.</meta>

<page-num>p. 222</page-num>

The seals must be made of specific metals: those of the chief kings should be made of gold; marquises should be made of silver; dukes of copper; prelates of tin or silver; knights of lead; presidents of fixed mercury <note>original: "fixed quicksilver"; in alchemy, this refers to mercury that has been "solidified" or stabilized through chemical processes</note>; and earls of silver and copper in equal parts. The entire group <note>original: "cohort"</note> is governed by AMAYMON, King of the East; CORSON, King of the West; ZIMINAR, King of the North; and GAAP, King of the South. 

<image-desc>The "Double Seal of Solomon" is a complex geometric diagram featuring a hexagram (six-pointed star) inscribed within a circle. A small cross is positioned at the top and bottom of the outer circle. The word "Tetragrammaton" <note>The sacred four-letter name of God in Hebrew, YHVH</note> is distributed around the seal: the top vertex of the hexagram is capped with a small circle containing "Te", while the triangles formed by the other points contain "tra" (top-left), "gram" (top-right), "ma" (bottom-left), and "ton" (bottom-right). The horizontal points of the hexagram contain the words "Alpha" <note>The first letter of the Greek alphabet, meaning "the beginning"</note> and "Omega" <note>The last letter of the Greek alphabet, meaning "the end"</note>. Stylized script letters are placed at the intersections of the star and the circle. In the central hexagon of the star, there is a symbol resembling a table or altar with two circles on top, and the letters "I H V" <note>The first three letters of the Tetragrammaton</note> written below it.</image-desc>

->THE DOUBLE SEAL OF SOLOMON<-

These four kings may be bound from 9:00 A.M. until noon; however, except on occasions of great importance, they should not be invoked directly, as they usually act through their subordinates. 

Marquises may be bound from 3:00 P.M. until 9:00 P.M. and from sunset until sunrise; dukes from sunrise until noon, provided the weather is clear; prelates at any hour of the day; knights only from dawn until sunrise, or from 4:00 P.M. until sunset; presidents at any daylight hour up to the evening twilight; counts and earls at any hour of the day, but the ritual must take place in woods and forests, or in a location that is free from noise and far from the presence of people.

# THE FIRST CONJURATION

*I invoke and conjure you, O Spirit [Name] <note>The "N." in the original text stands for "Nomen," Latin for "Name"; the practitioner would insert the name of the specific spirit they are calling</note>, and, fortified with the power of the Supreme Majesty, I strongly command you by BARALAMENSIS, BALDACHIENSIS, PAUMACHIE, APOLORESEDES and the most potent princes GENIO and LIACHIDE, Ministers of the Tartarean Seat <note>original: "Tartarean Seat," referring to Tartarus, the deepest abyss of the underworld in Greek mythology</note>, chief princes of the seat of APOLOGIA in the ninth region. I exorcise and command you, O Spirit [Name], by Him who spoke and it was done, and by the most holy and glorious names:*

->**ADONAI, EL, ELOHIM, ELOHE, ZEBAOTH, ELION, ESCHERCE, JAH, TETRAGRAMMATON, SHADDAI** <note>These are various Hebrew names for God: Adonai (Lord), El/Elohim/Elohe (God), Zebaoth (Hosts), Elion (Most High), Jah (a shortened form of Yahweh), Tetragrammaton (the four-letter name YHWH), and Shaddai (Almighty)</note><-

*Appear forthwith and show yourself to me here before this circle in a fair and human shape, without any deformity or horror. Come immediately from whatever part of the world you are in and give rational answers to my questions. Come presently, come visibly, come affably, and manifest that which I desire, being conjured by the name of the Eternal, Living, and True God, HELIOREM. I conjure you also by the particular and true name of your God to whom you owe your obedience, and by the name of the King who rules over you; come without delay! Come, fulfill my desires, and persist until the end according to my intentions.*

*I conjure you by Him to whom all creatures are obedient, by this ineffable name, **TETRAGRAMMATON JEHOVAH**, by which the elements are overthrown, the air is shaken, the sea turns back, the fire is generated, the earth moves, and all the hosts of things celestial, terrestrial, and infernal tremble and are confounded together. Speak to me visibly and affably in a clear, intelligible voice, free from ambiguity. Come therefore in the name **ADONAI ZEBAOTH**; come, why do you delay? **ADONAI SHADDAI**, King of kings, commands you.*

If this is repeated frequently and the spirit still does not appear, say the following:

<page-num>229</page-num>

# THE SECOND CONJURATION

*I invoke, conjure, and command you, O Spirit [Name], to appear and show yourself visibly before this circle in a fair and comely shape, without deformity or guile, by the name of **ON**; by the names **Y** and **V**, which Adam heard and spoke; by the name **JOTH** <note>original: "JOTH," referring to the Hebrew letter Yod</note>, which Jacob learned from the Angel on the night of his wrestling and was delivered from the hands of his brother Esau; by the name of God **AGLA** <note>A common magical acronym for the Hebrew phrase "Atah Gibor Le-olam Adonai," meaning "You are mighty forever, O Lord"</note>, which Lot heard and was saved with his family; by the name **ANEXHEXETON**, which Aaron spoke and was made wise; by the name **SCHEMES AMATHIA**, which Joshua invoked and the Sun stayed upon its course; by the name **EMMANUEL**, which the three children—Shadrach, Meshach, and Abednego—chanted in the midst of the fiery furnace and were delivered; by the name **ALPHA** and **OMEGA** <note>Greek: The first and last letters of the alphabet, symbolizing the beginning and the end</note>, which Daniel uttered and destroyed Bel and the Dragon; by the name **ZEBAOTH**, which Moses named, and all the rivers and waters in the land of Egypt brought forth frogs, which ascended into the houses of the Egyptians, destroying everything...*

*...things; by the Name ESCERCHIE ARISTON <note>A magical name, likely a corruption of Hebrew or Greek</note>, which Moses also named, and the rivers and waters in the land of Egypt were turned into blood; by the Name ELION <note>Hebrew: "Most High"</note>, upon which Moses called, and there fell a great hail, such as was never seen since the creation of the world; by the Name ADONAI <note>Hebrew: "Lord"</note>, which Moses named, and there came up locusts over all the land of Egypt and devoured what the hail had left; by the Name HOLY <note>original: "HAGIOS"</note>, by the Seal of ADONAI, by those others, which are JETROS, ATHENOROS, COMFORTER <note>original: "PARACLETUS"; a title for the Holy Spirit</note>; by the three Holy and Secret Names, AGLA, ON, TETRAGRAMMATON; by the dreadful Day of Judgment; by the changing Sea of Glass which is before the face of the Divine Majesty, mighty and powerful; by the four beasts before the Throne, having eyes before and behind; by the fire which is about the Throne, by the Holy Angels of Heaven, by the High Wisdom of God; by the Seat of BASDATHEA, by this Name PRIMEMATUM, which Moses named, and the earth opened and swallowed Korah, Dathan and Abiram <note>A reference to the biblical story in Numbers 16 where the earth consumed those who rebelled against Moses's authority</note>; do you give faithful answers to all my demands, and perform all my desires, so far as your office shall permit. Come therefore peaceably and graciously; come visibly and without delay; manifest that which I desire; speak with a clear and intelligible voice, that I may understand you.*

If the spirit does not come at the reciting of these Conjurations, as without doubt he will, proceed as follows, this being a Constraint:

### THIRD CONJURATION

*I conjure you, O spirit [Name] <note>original: "N."</note>, by all the most glorious and effective Names of the Great and Incomparable Lord the God of Hosts, come quickly and without delay, from whatever part of the world you are in; make rational answers to my demands; come visibly, speak graciously, and speak intelligibly to my understanding. I conjure and constrain you, O Spirit [Name], by all the aforesaid Names, as also by those seven other Names with which Solomon bound you and your companions in the brass vessels <note>Refers to the legend that King Solomon imprisoned 72 rebellious spirits in a brass vessel and sealed it with his magical seal</note> namely: ADONAI, PRERAI, TETRAGRAMMATON, ANEXHEXETON, INESSENSATOAL, PATHUMATON and ITEMON; manifest yourself before this circle and fulfill my will in all things that may seem good to me.* 

*If you are disobedient and refuse to come, then by the power of the Supreme Being, the everlasting Lord, that God Who created you and me, the whole world, and all contained within it in the space of six days; by EHEIEH <note>original: "EYE"; Hebrew: "I Am"</note>, by SHADDAI <note>original: "SARAY"; Hebrew: "Almighty"</note>, by the virtue of the Name PRIMEMATUM, which commands the whole host of Heaven; be disobedient, and behold, I will curse you and deprive you of your office, your joy, and your place. I will bind you in the depths of the bottomless pit, there to remain until the Day of the Last Judgment. I will chain you in the Lake of Eternal Fire, in the Lake of Fire and Brimstone, unless you come quickly, appearing before this circle to do my will. Come, therefore, in the Holy Names ADONAI, ZEBAOTH, AMIORAM; come, ADONAI commands you.*

Should the spirit still fail to appear, you may be sure that he has been sent by his King to some other place. Therefore, invoke the King to dispatch his servant as follows:
