<meta>This page continues the rituals of the *Lemegeton* (the Lesser Key of Solomon), specifically the "Goetia." After the initial conjurations have failed, the magician turns to higher authorities—first by appealing to the King who rules the spirit, and then by issuing a formal curse involving symbolic torture with fire and foul-smelling substances.</meta>

## INVOCATION OF THE KING

> O you great and powerful King AMAYMON, who rules by the power of the Supreme God, EL <note>Hebrew: "God"</note>, over all spirits, superior and inferior, but especially over the Infernal Order in the Realm <note>original: "Dominion"</note> of the East: I invoke and command you by the particular and true Name of God, by the God whom you worship, and by the Seal of your creation. I call you by the most mighty and powerful Name of God, JEHOVAH TETRAGRAMMATON <note>The four-letter Hebrew name of God, YHWH, considered too sacred to pronounce</note>, who cast you out of Heaven along with the rest of the Infernal Spirits. I invoke you by all the other potent and great Names of God, Creator of Heaven, Earth, and Hell, and everything contained within them; by their powers and virtues; and by the Name PRIMEMATUM <note>Likely a Latin-derived title meaning "the First Principle"</note>, which commands the entire host of Heaven. 
> 
> Force and compel the Spirit [Name] to appear here before this circle in a fair and handsome shape, without causing injury to me or to any other creature. Let him give me true and faithful answers so that I may accomplish my desired goal, whatever it may be, provided it is within the duties of his office. I command this by the power of God, EL, who has created and manages all things—celestial, aerial, terrestrial, and infernal.

Having invoked the King two or three times in this manner, conjure the spirit again using the previous forms, repeating them several times, and he will surely come. Yet, if he still fails to appear, be convinced that he is bound with chains in hell and is not in the custody of his King. To set him free, it is necessary to recite:

## THE CHAIN CURSE

> O you wicked and disobedient [Name], because you have not obeyed or respected the words which I have recited—the glorious and incomprehensible Names of the true God, Maker of all things in the world—now I, by the power of these Names which no creature can resist, curse you into the depths of the Bottomless Pit. There you shall remain until the Day of Doom, in the Hell of unquenchable fire and sulfur <note>original: "brimstone"</note>, unless you immediately appear in this triangle, before this circle, to do my will. Come, therefore, quickly and peaceably, by the Names ADONAI, ZEBAOTH, ADONAI, AMIORAM <note>Hebrew: "Lord of Hosts," followed by a magical variant of the same</note>. Come, come, ADONAI, the King who commands you!

Having read this far, if he still does not come, write his seal on parchment and put it in a black box with sulfur, asafoetida <note>A resin known for its extremely pungent, garlic-like, and unpleasant odor</note>, and other foul-smelling perfumes. Bind the box with iron wire, hang it on the point of your sword, and hold it over a charcoal fire which has been placed toward the direction from which the spirit is expected to come. Say first to the fire:

*I conjure you, O Fire, by Him who made you and all other creatures in the world, to torment, burn, and consume this Spirit [Name] everlastingly.*

## TO THE SPIRIT

<meta>This page continues the ritual instructions from the Goetia. Having previously called upon the King of the region to compel a stubborn spirit, the magician now moves to a formal curse and "excommunication" of the spirit, involving the symbolic burning of its seal, before the spirit finally appears and is welcomed.</meta>

> Because you are disobedient and do not obey my commandments or the precepts of the Lord your God, I—who am the servant of the Most High and Imperial Lord God of Armies <note>original: "Hosts"</note>, JEHOVAH, having His celestial power and permission—now, because of your resistance and contempt, your great disobedience and rebellion, will excommunicate you. I will destroy your name and seal, which I have in this box; I will burn them with unquenchable fire and bury them in endless oblivion, unless you come immediately, visibly and approachably <note>original: "affably"</note>, here before this circle, within this triangle, assuming a fair and attractive <note>original: "comely"</note> form, without doing harm to myself or any creature whatsoever, but giving reasonable answers to my requests and performing my desires in all things.

If he does not appear at this point, say as follows:

> You are still wicked <note>original: "pernicious"</note> and disobedient, refusing to appear and inform me about that which I desire to know. Now, therefore, in the Name and by the power and dignity of the

<page-num>233</page-num>

> All-powerful <note>original: "Omnipotent"</note> and Immortal Lord God of Armies, JEHOVAH TETRAGRAMMATON <note>Greek: "Four-lettered name," referring to the sacred Hebrew name of God, YHWH</note>, sole Creator of Heaven, Earth, and Hell, with all contained within them, the marvelous Arranger of all things visible and invisible: I do hereby curse and deprive you of all your office, power, and place. I bind you in the depth of the Bottomless Pit <note>The Abyss</note>, there to remain until the Day of Judgment, in the Lake of Fire and Sulfur <note>original: "Brimstone"</note> prepared for the rebellious Spirits. May all the Company of Heaven curse you; may the Sun, the Moon, the Stars, and the Light of the Host of Heaven curse you into unquenchable fire and unspeakable torments. And even as your name and seal are bound up in this box, to be choked with sulfurous and stinking substances and to burn in this physical <note>original: "material"</note> fire, so—in the Name of JEHOVAH, and by the power and dignity of the three Names, TETRAGRAMMATON, ANEXHEXETON <note>A magical name of God used in conjuration</note>, and PRIMEMATUM <note>Latin: "The First Principle"</note>—may all these drive you, O disobedient Spirit [Name], into the Lake of Fire prepared for the damned and accursed Spirits. Stay there until the Day of Doom, remembered no more before the face of that God Who shall come to judge the living <note>original: "quick"</note> and the dead, and the whole world, by fire.

Set the box in the flame, at which point he will appear. When he comes, put out the fire, create sweet perfumes <note>incense</note>, and give him a good reception <note>original: "entertainment"</note>. Show him the pentacle on the hem of your robe <note>original: "vestment"</note>, which should be covered with a linen cloth, and say:

*Behold your shame <note>original: "confusion"</note> if you are disobedient. Behold the Pentacle of Solomon which I have brought into your presence. Behold the person of the Exorcist, who is called OCTINIMOES <note>A mystical name assumed by the magician for the ritual</note>, in the midst of the Exorcism; I am armed by God and fearless, powerfully invoking and calling you. Therefore, make reasonable answers to my demands. Be obedient to me, your Master, in the Name of the Lord BATHAL <note>A barbarous name of power</note>, rushing upon ABRAC, ABEOR, and coming upon BEROR <note>Mystical words used to command the spirit's obedience</note>.*

<page-num>234</page-num>

He will become obedient and tell you to ask what you wish, for he is made subject by God to your purpose. When he shows himself humble and meek, say:

*Welcome, Spirit (or, most noble King), you are welcome to me. I have called you through Him Who created Heaven, Earth, and Hell, with all contained within them, and you have obeyed; and by that same power...*

<summary>This page details the "Spirit's Chain," a final curse used to force a defiant spirit to appear by symbolically burning its seal in a box of foul substances. Once the spirit manifests, the magician reveals the Pentacle of Solomon and assumes a ritual identity to command the spirit's total obedience.</summary>

<meta>This page concludes the ritual of the Goetia, providing the final binding of the spirit and the "License to Depart," which allows the spirit to leave safely. It then transitions into a critical commentary by Waite regarding the historical sources and the anachronistic Christian elements found in these supposedly ancient Solomonic texts.</meta>

*...bind you to remain approachably <note>original: "affably"</note> and visibly before this circle, within this triangle, as long as I need you, and not to depart without my permission <note>original: "license"</note>, until you have truly and faithfully fulfilled all that I shall require.*

## *THE LICENSE TO DEPART*

*O Spirit [Name], because you have diligently answered my demands, I hereby give you permission <note>original: "license"</note> to depart, without injury to man or beast. Depart, I say, and be willing and ready to come whenever you are duly exorcised and conjured <note>OCR original: "con red"</note> by the Sacred Rites of Magic. I command <note>original: "conjure"</note> you to withdraw peaceably and quietly, and may the peace of God continue forever between me and you. Amen.*

Do not leave the circle until he has gone, but say prayers to God for the great blessing He has bestowed upon you by thus granting your desires and delivering you from the malice of the enemy <note>The "enemy" here refers to the demonic spirit or the devil</note>.

In place of these wordy <note>original: "verbose"</note> and tiresome conjurations, the Latin version by Johann Weyer <note>original: "Wierus"; Weyer was a 16th-century physician and occultist who criticized the witch hunts</note> substitutes one general form of summoning <note>original: "citation"</note> in the Name of the Christian Trinity. This is not actually a specific anachronism <note>An "anachronism" is something placed in the wrong historical time period</note>, because the compiler of the *Lemegeton* <note>The Lesser Key of Solomon</note> was familiar at least with the books of the New Testament. This is proven by the references in the Second Conjuration to the "Living Creatures of the Apocalypse" <note>Referring to the four winged creatures described in the Book of Revelation 4:6–8</note>. This leaves the attribution of the work to King Solomon in exactly the state that our respect for a prince of Israel would naturally desire <note>Waite is being sarcastic here, implying that Solomon could not have written a book referencing the New Testament</note>. No doubt, however, the English editor of the *Greater Key of Magic* <note>original: "The Key Major of Magic," referring to the Clavicula Salomonis</note> would see no reason to question the attribution and would explain the anachronism very simply as a later addition <note>original: "interpolation"</note>.

<image-desc>A woodcut illustration showing a "Hell Mouth"—a large, stylized monstrous head with gaping jaws. Within the mouth are several figures. Below the monster's lower jaw, a horned figure is shown lying on the ground, bound by heavy chains.</image-desc>

->HELL MOUTH AND THE CHAINED DEVIL.<-
->*From the Cædmon manuscript.*<- <note>Refers to the Junius manuscript, an 11th-century collection of Anglo-Saxon biblical poetry formerly attributed to the poet Cædmon.</note>

221:1 Numerous experiments by alchemists are concerned with solidifying <note>original: "congealing"</note> and fixing mercury <note>original: "quicksilver"</note>, but there are also "Kabbalistic" <note>original: "cabalistic"</note> processes by which this substance was prepared specifically for the construction of talismans. These must not be confused with the more practical <note>original: "sober"</note>—if not necessarily more successful—recipes of the Hermetic Art <note>The philosophical and magical tradition attributed to Hermes Trismegistus, often involving alchemy</note>. On this point, see *The Secrets of Little Albert* <note>original: "Les Secrets du Petit Albert"</note>. Lyons edition, 1775, pp. 63-65.

222:1 The chief consecrations of the magic circle are summarized by Francis Barrett in *The Key to Ceremonial Magic* as follows: 
> Therefore, when you wish to consecrate any place or circle, you should use the Prayer of Solomon used in the dedication and consecration of the Temple; you must likewise bless the place by sprinkling it with holy water and with incense <note>original: "suffumigations"</note>, and commemorate holy mysteries in the blessing, such as these: the sanctification of the throne of God, of Mount Sinai, of the Tabernacle of the Covenant, and of the Holy of Holies. . . . And by invoking all Divine Names which are significant to this.

225:1 I do not think that an intentional disrespect <note>original: "profanation"</note> of the sacraments and holy offices of the Church was intended here. This would be ascribing a depth of motive to the Grimoires <note>Textbooks of magic used to summon spirits</note> which they never actually possessed.

226:1 ALTERNATE VERSION: 
> *Amor, Amacor, Amides, Theodanias, Anitor*; by the merits of the angels, O Lord, I will put on the garment of salvation, so that I may bring what I desire to fulfillment <note>original: "effects"</note> through You, the Most Holy Lord <note>original: "Adonai"</note>, Whose Kingdom endures for ever and ever. Amen.

# CHAPTER V

## Concerning the Mystery of the Holy Kingdom <note>original: "Sanctum Regnum"</note>, or the Government of Evil Spirits; being the Rite of Conjuration according to the *Grimorium Verum* <note>The True Grimoire</note>

The preparations of the practitioner <note>original: "operator"</note> prescribed by the *Grimorium Verum* are fully described in the earlier chapters, but they may be summarized here as follows: 
1. A strict fast of three days; 
2. Retreat or isolation from human society; 
3. Abstinence from sexual intercourse; 
4. The recitation of certain prayers and the performance of certain ritual washings <note>original: "ablutions"</note>; 
5. The construction of the required instruments. 

He must further devote himself to the invocations, so that his memory does not fail him at the moment set apart for the work, and to ensure he has mastered the practice. He should also recite the following prayer daily <u>1</u>—once at Prime, twice at Tierce, three times at Sext, four times at Nones, five times at Vespers, and six times before going to rest. It is explained that these hours are "planetary" and of unequal length <note>In traditional astrology, planetary hours divide the day and night into 12 equal segments each, meaning the length of an "hour" changes depending on the season</note>. <u>2</u> Prime is calculated at sunrise, Tierce at three hours after, Sext at midday, Nones at three in the afternoon, and Vespers at sunset.

<summary>This page introduces the specific requirements for the "Sanctum Regnum" ritual from the Grimorium Verum, detailing the ascetic preparations and the schedule of daily prayers based on planetary hours.</summary>
<keywords>Grimorium Verum, Sanctum Regnum, planetary hours, Francis Barrett, consecration, ritual preparation, alchemy, mercury, Petit Albert</keywords>

--- Page 197 ---
<meta>This page continues the instructions for the "Sanctum Regnum" or Holy Kingdom ritual from the *Grimorium Verum*. It transitions from the general preparation of the magician to the specific technical requirements for summoning the spirit Scirlin and the superior demons, Lucifer and Beelzebuth.</meta>

ASTRACHIOS, ASACH, ASARCA, ABEDUMABAL, SILAT, ANABOTAS, JESUBILIN, SCIGIN, GENEON, DOMOL <note>These are "barbarous names of evocation," phonetic strings of words believed to carry magical power through their sound rather than their literal meaning.</note>, Lord God, who dwells above the heavens, whose gaze searches the depths <note>original: "abyss"</note>, grant me, I pray to You, the power to conceive in my mind and to carry out that which I desire to do. I hope to attain this end through Your help, O God Almighty, who lives and reigns forever and ever. Amen.

Once these things are done, says the Grimoire <note>A textbook of magic</note>, it remains only to follow the invocations and to draw <note>original: "compose"</note> the symbols <note>original: "characters"</note>. To this end, the practitioner <note>original: "operator"</note> shall proceed as follows: On the day and in the hour of Mars <note>In planetary magic, each day and hour is ruled by a planet; Mars typically represents strength or conflict</note>, while the Moon is waxing <note>increasing in light</note>, and at the first hour of the day—which begins automatically fifteen minutes before sunrise—a piece of virgin parchment <note>Parchment made from the skin of an unborn or young animal</note> must be prepared. It must contain all the symbols and invocations of the spirits one wishes to call upon. 

On the day and in the hour mentioned above, the practitioner should bind the little finger of the left hand (which is the finger associated with Mercury) with a thread woven by a virgin girl. Let him pierce that finger with the surgical knife <note>original: "lancet"</note> of the Art <note>A ritual tool used in ceremonial magic</note> to draw blood. With this blood, he must draw the symbol of Scirlin, upon whom all other spirits depend. Scirlin is their messenger and can force them to appear even against their will, because he has been granted the power of the Emperor. His invocation, which must be written down, is as follows:

### THE INVOCATION OF SCIRLIN

HELON, TAUL, VARF, PAN, HEON, HOMONOREUM, CLEMIALH, SERUGEATH, AGLA, TETRAGRAMMATON, CASOLY.

Due to the confusion so common in these Grimoires, the symbol for Scirlin is not actually provided. Consequently, any practitioner who followed the instructions of this ritual would have had to turn to one of the superior spirits instead. However, these instructions are not described clearly for the uninitiated <note>original: "ad clerum," a Latin phrase meaning "to the clergy," implying a text meant only for those with specialized knowledge</note>, and it is almost impossible to figure out a logical method of procedure from them. The practitioner's first and last name must apparently be written below the spirit's symbol in a prescribed manner, which is also omitted; the conjuration of the spirit must be added and incense burned in his honor.

### CONJURATION TO LUCIFER

LUCIFER, OUYAR, CHAMERON, ALISEON, MANDOUSIN, PREMY, ORIET, NAYDRUS, ESMONY, EPARINESONT, ESTIOT, DUMOSSON, DANOCHAR, CASMIEL, HAYRAS, FABELLERONTHON, SODIRNO, PEATHAM, *Come*, LUCIFER. AMEN.

### CONJURATION FOR BEELZEBUTH

BEELZEBUTH, LUCIFER, MADILON, SOLYMO, SAROY, THEU, AMECLO, SEGRAEL, PRAREDUN, ADRICANOROM, MARTIRO, TIMO, CAMERON, PHORSY, METOSITE, PRUMOSY, DUMASO, ELIVISA, ALPHROIS, FUBENTRONTY, *Come*, BEELZEBUTH. AMEN.

## CONJURATION FOR ASTAROTH

ASTAROTH, ADOR, CAMESO, VALUERITUF, MARESO, LODIR, CADOMIR, ALUIEL, CALNISO, TELY, PLEORIM, VIORDY, CUREVIORBAS, CAMERON, VESTURIEL, VULNAVII, BENEZ, MEUS CALMIRON, NOARD, NISA CHENIBRANBO CALEVODIUM, BRAZO, TABRASOL <note>These are "barbarous names," rhythmic or phonetic strings of words used in magic rituals to focus the practitioner's intent and command spiritual forces.</note>, *Come*, ASTAROTH. AMEN.

After repeating this conjuration seven times to one of these superior spirits, the spirit will appear immediately to carry out whatever you desire. NOTE.—This conjuration must be written on virgin paper <term>virgin paper: paper or parchment that has never been used or marked before the ritual</term> or parchment before you call upon the spirits. Once the spirits have fulfilled your requests, they may be dismissed by using the following:

### DISCHARGE

> *Go in peace to your own places; let peace be with you, and be ready to come whenever I call you, in the Name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit. Amen.*

## CONJURATION FOR INFERIOR SPIRITS

O SURMY, DELMUSAN, ATALSLOYM, CHARUSIHOA, MELANY, LIAMINTHO, COLEHON, PARON, MADOIN, MERLOY, BULERATOR, DONMEO, HONE, PELOYM, IBASIL, MEON, ALYMDRICTELS, PERSON, CRISOLSAY, LEMON SEFLE NIDAR HORIEL PEUNT, HALMON, ASOPHIEL, ILNOSTREON, BANIEL, VERMIAS, ESLEVOR, NOELMA, DORSAMOT, LHAVALA, OMOT, FRANGAM, BELDOR, DRAGIN, *Come*.

The name of the specific spirit you need must be added here on the parchment. He will then appear—unless unforeseen circumstances prevent it—and will grant what you desire from him—that is to say, if it is meant to be. Afterward, he may be dismissed using the previous Discharge.

The symbols <note>original: "characters"</note> should be burned in each case, as they are effective for only one use.

### ANOTHER CONJURATION

> *I call upon you, [Name], by the great living God, the Sovereign Creator of all things, to appear in a pleasant human form, without noise and without causing fear, to answer truly all the questions I shall ask you. I command you to do this by the power of these Holy and Sacred Names.*

# CHAPTER VI

## The Mysteries of Infernal Evocation <term>evocation: the act of calling upon or summoning a spirit, especially a demon</term> according to the Grand Grimoire

### § 1. The Rite of Lucifuge <note>Lucifuge Rofocale: In the Grand Grimoire, he is the demon in charge of Hell's government and treasury, often identified as a high-ranking lieutenant of Lucifer.</note>

The *Grand Grimoire* shares with the *Grimoire of Honorius* the dark distinction of containing a clear and complete ritual of Black Magic. Each in its own way is indeed an...

<summary>This page introduces the Grand Grimoire's section on summoning demons, specifically the Rite of Lucifuge, while providing scholarly corrections for corrupted magical names and notes on ritual timing.</summary>
<keywords>Grand Grimoire, Lucifuge Rofocale, Infernal Evocation, Black Magic, Key of Solomon, ritual timing, barbarous names, Lincoln Imp</keywords>

--- Page 200 ---
<meta>This page continues the introduction to the Grand Grimoire, focusing on the specific preparations and the initial sacrifice required before summoning a spirit.</meta>

...is an exceedingly curious work. In the first part, it contains what is probably the only printed method for making pacts with spirits. The second part is remarkable, firstly because of its alleged origin and the detailed way it is presented, and secondly for the ecclesiastical <note>ecclesiastical: relating to the Christian Church or its clergy</note> nature of its ritual process. This process could hardly have failed to fool some gullible and priestly sorcerers of the uneducated sort, assuming they were willing to disgrace their ordination vows. Both parts require, in a study of this kind, to be printed almost in full <note>original: "in extenso"</note>. Setting aside their introductory sections, which have already been sufficiently covered, they are provided almost word-for-word <note>original: "verbatim"</note> in the chapters that follow.

It should be remembered that the operator, or Karcist <term>Karcist: A title used in the Grand Grimoire for the master of the ritual, or the chief operator.</term> as he is called in the *Grand Grimoire*, is advised to practice self-restraint, fasting, and similar hardships for a full quarter of the moon, coinciding with the first quarter of the moon's cycle. On the morning following the first night of the quarter, he must go to a druggist and purchase a bloodstone, called *Ematille* (that is, Hematite <note>original: "Hæmatites"</note>). He must carry this on him constantly for fear of accident, and in anticipation that the spirit he intends to control and bind will from then on do everything in his power to overwhelm the operator with terror. The spirit does this to incite him to abandon the task, hoping in this way to escape from the traps that are beginning to be woven around him.

The next step is the purchase of a virgin kid <note>virgin kid: a young goat that has not yet mated</note>, which must be beheaded on the third day of the moon. Before the sacrifice, a garland of vervain <term>vervain: A medicinal herb often associated with protection and purification in magic.</term> must be tied around the animal's neck, just below the head, and secured with a green ribbon. The sacrifice must be offered at the site of the upcoming summoning <note>original: "evocation"</note>, a lonely and isolated spot free from any interruptions. There, with his right arm bare to the shoulder, having armed himself with a blade of fine steel and having lit a fire of white wood, the Karcist shall recite the following words in a hopeful and lively manner:

### *INITIAL OFFERING*

> *I sacrifice <note>original: "immolate"</note> this victim to You, O great ADONAY, ELOIM, ARIEL, and JEHOVAM, to the honor, glory, and power of Your Name, which is superior to all spirits. O great ADONAY! Deign to receive it as an acceptable offering. Amen.*

At this point, he must cut the throat of the kid, skin it, place the body on the fire, and burn it to ashes. These ashes must be collected and thrown toward the rising sun, while repeating the following words: *It is to the honor, glory, and dominion of Your Name, O great ADONAY, ELOIM, ARIEL, and JEHOVAM, that I spill the blood of this victim! Deign, O You great ADONAY, to receive its ashes as an acceptable sacrifice.*

While the victim is being consumed by the flames, the

<summary>This page describes the preliminary rituals of the Grand Grimoire, including the acquisition of a bloodstone for protection and the ritual sacrifice of a virgin kid to the names of God.</summary>
<keywords>Grand Grimoire, Karcist, Hematite, sacrifice, Adonay, Eloim, Ariel, Jehovam, vervain, ritual preparation</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the instructions for the Grand Grimoire's ritual of summoning, specifically detailing the items needed and the physical construction of the protective Kabbalistic circle.</meta>

The operator shall rejoice in the honor and glory of the great ADONAY, ELOIM, ARIEL, and JEHOVAM <note>ADONAY, ELOIM, ARIEL, JEHOVAM: Various Hebrew names and titles for God frequently used in magical invocations to command spirits.</note>, taking care to preserve the skin of the virgin kid to form the round or grand Kabalistic circle, in which he must stand on the day of the supreme enterprise.

The sacrifice of the victim is followed by the selection and composition of the Blasting Rod, which has already been described. From the purpose for which it is intended, and from the silence of the Grimoire on the matter, it may be inferred that the preparation of the goatskin is exceedingly simple and does not involve the removal of the hair.

When the night of action has arrived, the operator shall gather up his rod, goatskin, and the stone called *Ematille* <term>Ematille: Hematite, or bloodstone; a mineral often used in magic for protection and grounding.</term>. He shall further provide himself with two vervain crowns, two candlesticks, and two candles of virgin wax <note>virgin wax: wax that has never been used for any other purpose, signifying purity.</note>, made by a young girl and duly blessed. Let him also take a new steel and two new flints, with sufficient tinder to kindle a fire, as well as half a bottle of brandy, some blessed incense and camphor, and four nails from the coffin of a deceased child. All these must be carried to the place chosen for the Great Work, where everything laid down hereafter must be scrupulously performed and the dread Kabalistic circle must be drawn in an accurate manner. Lastly, it must be carefully borne in mind that there should be either one or three people taking part in the ceremony, including the Karcist <term>Karcist: The master of the ritual or the lead operator who communicates with the spirits.</term>, who is the person appointed to address the spirit while holding the Destroying Rod in his hand. The summoning process may be given in the actual words of the Grimoire.

## CONCERNING A TRUE REPRESENTATION OF THE GRAND KABALISTIC CIRCLE

You must begin by forming a circle with strips of the kid's skin, fastened to the ground by means of your four nails. Then with the stone called *Ematille*, you must trace the triangle within the circle, beginning at the eastern point. A large A, a small e, a small a, and a small j must be drawn in a similar manner, as also the sacred name of Jesus between two crosses. By this means, the spirits will have no power to harm you from behind. The Karcist and his assistants may then fearlessly proceed to

## THE GRAND KABBALISTIC CIRCLE

[The Karcist and his assistants must take] their places within the triangle and, regardless of any noises they may hear, they should set the two candlesticks and the two vervain <note>vervain: A plant traditionally used in folk medicine and magic, often associated with protection and purification.</note> crowns on the right and left sides of the triangle within the circle. Once this is done, the two candles may be lighted. The operator must ensure there is a new brazier <note>brazier: A portable heater or grill used to hold burning coals.</note> in front of the Karcist, filled with freshly consecrated charcoal. This fire must be kindled by the Karcist, who pours a small amount of brandy and a portion of the camphor into it; the rest of the camphor should be kept to feed the fire periodically, depending on how long the ritual lasts. Having precisely performed everything mentioned above, the chief operator may repeat the following prayer:

"I present to You, O great ADONAY, this incense as the purest I can obtain; in the same way, I present to You this charcoal prepared from the most ethereal of woods. I offer them, O grand and omnipotent ADONAY, ELOIM, ARIEL, and JEHOVAM, with my whole soul and my whole heart. Deign, O great ADONAY, to receive them as an acceptable burnt offering <note>original: holocaust; here used in its traditional sense of a religious animal sacrifice or offering completely consumed by fire.</note>. Amen."

->THE GREAT CIRCLE OF WHITE MAGIC<-

You should also be careful, says the <term>Grimoire</term> <note>Grimoire: A textbook of magic providing instructions on how to create magical objects and summon spirits.</note>, to have no mixed metal <note>original: "alloyed metal"</note> on your person, except for a gold or silver coin wrapped in paper. You must throw this to the spirit when he appears outside the circle to prevent him from harming you. While he is picking up the coin, immediately begin the following prayer, strengthening yourself with courage, energy, and caution. Be also especially careful that the <term>Karcist</term> <note>Karcist: The lead magician or operator who directs the ritual and is the only one permitted to speak to the spirits.</note> is the sole speaker; the assistants must remain completely silent, even if they are questioned or threatened by the spirit.

---

### *FIRST PRAYER*

> *O great and living God, existing in one and the same Being—the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit; I worship You with the deepest reverence, and I submit with the greatest confidence to Your holy and sufficient protection. I believe with the most sincere faith that You are my Creator, my Benefactor, my Preserver, and my Lord, and I testify to Your supreme Majesty that my only desire is to belong to You throughout all eternity. Amen.*

---

### *SECOND PRAYER*

> *O great and living God, Who created humanity to enjoy happiness in this life, Who adapted all things for our needs, and declared that everything should be subject to our will, look favorably upon this my plan. Do not allow the rebellious spirits to possess those treasures which were formed by Your own hands for our earthly requirements. Grant me, O great God, the power to command them by the potent and terrifying names in Your <term>Clavicle</term> <note>Clavicle: Derived from the Latin "Clavicula" meaning "Little Key"; refers here to the "Key of Solomon," a famous book of magical spells.</note>: ADONAY, ELOIM, ARIEL, JEHOVAM, TAGLA, MATHON, be favorable to me. Amen.*

# OFFERTORY

> I offer You this incense as the purest I have been able to obtain, O sublime <term>Adonay</term> <note>Adonay, Eloim, and Jehovam are transliterations of Hebrew names for God: Adonai (Lord), Elohim (God), and Jehovah (the Tetragrammaton YHWH).</note>, **Eloim**, **Ariel**, and **Jehovam**; graciously deign to receive it as an acceptable sacrificial offering <note>original: "holocaust"</note>. Lean toward me in Your power, and enable me to succeed in this great undertaking. So be it. Amen.

# FIRST CONJURATION

->Addressed to the Emperor Lucifer<-

> Emperor Lucifer, Master and Prince of Rebellious Spirits, I solemnly command you to leave your dwelling, in whatever part of the world it may be located, and come here to communicate with me. I command and I conjure you in the Name of the Mighty living God—Father, Son, and Holy Spirit—to appear without noise and without any foul smell, to respond in a clear and understandable voice, point by point, to all that I shall ask you. Otherwise, you shall most certainly be compelled to obedience by the Power of the divine **Adonay**, **Eloim**, **Ariel**, **Jehovam**, **Tagla**, **Mathon**, and by the entire hierarchy of superior intelligences, who shall force you against your will. Come! Come! <note>original Latin: "Venité, Venité!"</note> **Submiritillor Lucifuge** <note>Lucifuge Rofocale is often cited in grimoires as the prime minister of Hell; the name means "he who flies from the light."</note>, or eternal torment shall overwhelm you by the great Power of this **Blasting Rod** <note>The "Blasting Rod" or "Virge Foudroyante" is the magical wand described in the *Grand Grimoire* for controlling spirits.</note>. Immediately! <note>original Latin: "In subito."</note>

# SECOND CONJURATION

> I command and I solemnly urge you, Emperor Lucifer, as the representative of the mighty living God, and by the power of **Emanuel**, His only Son, Who is your master and mine, and by the virtue of His precious blood, which He shed to redeem mankind from your chains, I command you to leave your dwelling, wherever it may be. I swear that I will give you only fifteen minutes <note>original: "one quarter of an hour alone"</note> if you do not immediately come here and communicate with me in an audible and understandable voice. Or, if your personal presence is impossible, send me your Messenger **Astarôt** in a human form, without either noise or foul smell; failing which, I will strike you and your entire race with the terrible **Blasting Rod** into the depths of the bottomless pits. I do this by the Power of those great words in the **Clavicle** <note>The *Clavicula Salomonis* or *Key of Solomon*, a famous grimoire that serves as a foundation for much of ceremonial magic.</note>—By **Adonay**,

> **Eloim**, **Ariel**, **Jehovam**, **Tagla**, **Mathon**, **Almouzin**, **Arios**, **Pithona**, **Magots**, **Sylphs**, **Tabots**, **Salamanders**, **Gnomes**, of the **Earth**, of the **Heavens** <note>original Latin: "Cœlis"</note>, **Godens**, and of the **Water** <note>original Latin: "Aqua"</note>. Immediately! <note>original Latin: "In subito."</note>

### *Of the Manifestation of the Spirit*

*Look, I am here! What do you seek from me? Why do you disturb my rest? Strike me no more with that terrifying <note>original: "dread"</note> rod!*

### *Reply to the Spirit*

*If you had appeared when I first summoned <note>original: "invoked"</note> you, I would by no means have struck you; remember, if the request I make of you is refused, I am determined to torment you forever.*

### *The Spirit's Answer*

*Torment me no further. Say, instead, what you require of me.*

->LUCIFUGE ROFOCALE<-

### *The Requisition*

*I require that you communicate with me twice every night of the week, either with me or with those who are entrusted with my current Book, which you shall approve and sign; I allow you to choose whichever hours suit you, if you do not approve of those I now list. Namely <note>original: "To wit"</note>:*

*On Monday at nine o'clock and at midnight.*

*On Tuesday at ten o'clock and at one in the morning.*

*On Wednesday at eleven o'clock and at two in the morning.*

*On Thursday at eight and ten o'clock.*

*On Friday at seven in the evening and at midnight.*

*On Saturday at nine in the evening and at eleven at night.*

### *Answer and Compliance of the Spirit*

*On Monday at ten o'clock and at midnight.*

*On Tuesday at eleven o'clock and at one in the morning.*

*On Wednesday at midnight and at two in the morning.*

*On Thursday at eight and at eleven o'clock.*

*On Friday at nine o'clock and at midnight.*

*On Saturday at ten o'clock and at one in the morning.*

This is a <term>Gnome</term> <note>An elemental earth spirit traditionally believed to guard hidden gold and gems</note>, which the magician can drive away with the tip of his rod. Once he does so, the apparition will move toward the treasure. The Karcist must follow. Upon reaching the treasure, he will be astonished to see the person who originally hid it, who will also try to grab him but will be unable to even get near him.

moment as a guarantee. Before this, he must throw down a piece of his own money that he has bitten with his own teeth. After doing this, he may leave, walking backward and carrying away as much of the treasure as he can. The rest of the treasure cannot be lost to him if these precautions are followed.

However, he must be careful not to turn around, no matter what noise he hears; at this critical moment, it will truly seem as if all the mountains in the world are crashing down upon him. For this reason, he must be strengthened with extraordinary courage, fearing nothing and remaining perfectly steady. By acting this way, he will be led back by the spirit...

## § 2. Concerning the Genuine Holy Kingdom, or the True Method of Making Pacts
<note>original: "Sanctum Regnum," Latin for "Holy Kingdom," a term often used in magic to describe the mastery of the spiritual world</note>

There are two kinds of pacts, says the *True Grimoire* <note>original: "Grimorium Verum"</note>, the implicit and the explicit; but know at the same time that among the various kinds of spirits there are some which bind and some which do not bind, except in a very superficial manner. As for the first kind, they are those which require something personally belonging to you whenever a pact is agreed upon. Against these you must be on your guard, because the deceptive friend becomes an open enemy. 1 The *True Grimoire* does not,

<meta>p. 255</meta>

however, discuss at any greater length the question of a compact with Satan or his ministers, though it may perhaps be inferred from its pages that all dealings with evil spirits are based on a law of exchange. 1 They can be bent to the intention of the magician, but it is on the express understanding that they are satisfied on their part, because, as we have seen, these kinds of creatures give nothing for nothing.

<summary>This page concludes the ritual of the Grand Grimoire with a formal dismissal of Lucifer and a prayer of thanks to God. It begins a new section explaining that pacts with spirits are essentially business transactions based on the "law of exchange."</summary>

<keywords>Lucifer, Karcist, Blasting Rod, Adonay, Eloim, Ariel, Jehovah, Sanctum Regnum, Grimorium Verum, magic pacts, law of exchange</keywords>

--- Page 210 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion of the "Grand Grimoire," specifically focusing on the necessity and benefits of making pacts with spirits for those who lack the traditional magical tools like the Blasting Rod or a consecrated circle.</meta>

According to the author of the *Grand Grimoire*, the pact is a concession to the poverty of the practitioner's resources. In Black Magic, as in certain other processes, those in need must be ready to make sacrifices, and the sorcerer who is poorly equipped must ultimately pay a higher price. The genuine Holy Kingdom <note>original: "Sanctum Regnum"</note> of the Great Key <note>original: "Grand Clavicle"</note>, otherwise known as the Agreed Pacts of Demons <note>original: "Pacta Conventa Dæmonum"</note>, is explained by the *Grand Grimoire* for the information of those who wish to bind spirits but lack the necessary resources to create the Blasting Rod and Kabbalistic circle. Such people, it is claimed, will never succeed in summoning spirits unless they follow, point by point, everything detailed below concerning the method of making pacts with any spirit whatsoever. This applies whether the goal is the possession of treasures hidden in the earth, the enjoyment of women or girls and obtaining any desired favor from them, the discovery of the most hidden secrets in all royal courts and government offices <note>original: "Cabinets"</note> of the world, the revelation of the most impenetrable mysteries, or engaging a spirit to perform one's work during the

<page-num>256</page-num>

night; whether it is to cause hail to fall or a storm in any specific place, to open seals, to see what is happening in private houses and learn all the lore of the shepherds, to obtain the Hand of Glory <note>A macabre charm made from the dried and pickled hand of a hanged man, believed to have various magical properties like making people motionless</note> and discern all the qualities and virtues of metals, minerals, and vegetables, or of animals both pure and impure, and "to perform things so astounding that no living person could fail to be completely bewildered upon seeing that, by means of a pact with certain spirits, one can discover the greatest secrets of Nature, which are hidden from the eyes of all other men."

The *Grimoire* states that we owe the discovery of the genuine method of making pacts to the Great Key of the great King Solomon. He supposedly used this method himself to acquire his immense wealth, to enjoy his countless women, and to reveal the most impenetrable secrets <note>original: "arcana"</note> of Nature, through which every kind of good and evil may be achieved.

It does not seem possible to enter into a binding compact with the highest rulers of Hell <note>original: "Infernus"</note>—Lucifer, Beelzebub <note>original: "Beelzebuth"</note>, and Astaroth. The sorcerer must be satisfied with one of the six governors, <gloss>1</gloss> who will generally work through their agents. An essential condition of the pact should be a bargain for service from one of the three superiors among these specific subordinates.

Having decided to make a pact, continues the *Grand Grimoire*, you must begin on the previous evening by using a new and unused knife to cut a rod of wild hazel that has never borne fruit. This rod should be similar to the Blasting Rod previously described.

This must be done at the exact moment when the sun appears on our horizon. Once this is finished, arm yourself...

### Of the Spirit's Second Manifestation

> *Why do you torment me further? Leave me to rest, and I will give you the nearest treasure, on the condition that you set aside one coin for me on the first Monday of each month, and that you do not call me more than once a week—specifically between ten at night and two in the morning. ### Reply to the Spirit

> *I agree to your request, provided that you deliver the nearest treasure that I can carry away immediately.*

Follow the spirit without fear, throw your contract onto the hoard, touch it with your magic rod, take as much as you can carry, and return to the safety of the triangle by walking backward. Place the treasure in front of you and recite the Dismissal <note>original: "Discharge"</note> of the Spirit, as described in the previous ritual.

It will be seen from the wording of the contract that it is intentionally vague <note>original: "evasive"</note>, and the final response of the supposed spirit seems to recognize that it can only win if the sorcerer becomes negligent. But if there are courts of judgment in the underworld, the sorcerer might find it difficult to argue such a case against the <term>devil's advocate</term> <note>original: "advocatus diaboli," a term for a person appointed to argue against a claim in a formal inquiry, here used as an infernal prosecutor</note>. Lucifer and his deputies are credited with an awkward skill in taking possession of the defendant when the contract expires. There were apparently more binding contracts, and better ways of overriding them, for in the year 1678 the Abbé Eynatton published a *Manual of Exorcisms* which contains the following formal process for forcing the Demon to return any writing that constitutes an agreement with him:

### EXORCISM

## EXORCISM

*I exorcise you, O wicked Satan, who, though your power has faded, still pretend to impose a tyrant’s yoke on humanity. I exorcise you by Jesus Christ, Who came into this world for the salvation of sinners; I command <note>original: "conjure"</note> you to remove your yoke immediately from this creature, who, deceived by your tricks, formerly delivered himself to you. Abandoning you from this point forward, he commits himself to the Divine mercy, seeking the service of Him alone to whom he is indebted, Who also promises the reward of eternal life to those who follow Him. I exorcise you by the precious Blood of Jesus Christ, by which the legal judgment <note>original: "deed of judgment"</note> against us has been wiped away, so that when Jesus receives the sinner into His favor, you shall not dare to bring any charge against him, and you shall not fail to restore the document by which this creature bound himself to you, as it is now cancelled by the Blood of Jesus Christ.*

*Hear, therefore, accursed Satan, who are powerless over a servant of God when, encouraged by his true Lord, he turns to another service; you boast of this deed in vain; I command you to restore it in the name of the Lord, as a proof before the whole world that when God receives a sinner, you no longer have any rule over his soul. I command <note>original: "abjure"</note> you, by Him who expelled you from your stronghold, stripped you of the weapons which you trusted in, and distributed your spoils. Return, therefore, this document, by which this creature of God foolishly bound himself to your service; return it, I say, in His name—*

Between the cleverness of the sorcerer and the assistance of the Church in revoking such acts and deeds, there was little chance for Hell <note>original: "Infernus"</note>, and the reluctance of Lucifuge Rofocale to enter into the tricky contract is, on the whole, very understandable.

## Footnotes

254:1 Catholic theologians who have concerned themselves with the question of the pact have so extended the sphere of its operation that it includes the mere process of communicating with spirits. In his *Theological Dictionary*, Bergier defines the pact as an express or tacit agreement made with the demon in the hope of accomplishing things which transcend the powers of Nature. It is "express and formal" when the operator himself invokes and demands the help of the demon, whether that personage really appears in response, or the sorcerer merely believes that he sees him (that is to say, is hallucinating). It is also express and formal when the demon is invoked through the mediation of someone else supposed to be in contact with him; in other words, consulting a sorcerer is equivalent to making a compact with Satan. The performance of any act with the expectation of a result from the demon is another compact of this kind. The pact is "tacit or equivalent" when an act is performed with a view to some effect which cannot naturally follow, while the intervention of God is not to be expected. There remains only the fiend. For example, should anyone cure a disease by uttering certain words, this could only take place by the operation of the Infernal Spirit, because the words do not themselves possess the required power, and God is not likely to provide it. Hence, all theologians conclude that not only every species of Magic, but every kind of superstition involves at least a tacit and equivalent compact with the demon. St. Augustine and St. Thomas Aquinas are said to have taken this view. It is by precisely such judgments as these that the theology of the Middle Ages made itself a laughingstock <note>original: "by-word"</note>, and it is also for this reason that sorcery flourished most when such doctrines ruled. A power which condemns everything to the same penalty condemns nothing effectively, and that which is judged too harshly is always vindicated in the eyes of the people. We have come to see that horse-stealing is not murder, and we no longer punish it by the gallows; so also, with due respect to the masters of Theology, the follies of a village maiden who believes in a <gloss>p. 255</gloss> fortune-telling <note>original: "soothsaying"</note> gypsy, and the trickeries of a quack doctor who is absurd enough to take "Abracadabra" <note>An ancient magical formula or word used in amulets</note> seriously, are not the crime of Faust <note>The legendary German scholar who sold his soul to the devil</note>. But the learned Bishop of Hippo <note>St. Augustine</note> and the Angel of the Schools <note>St. Thomas Aquinas</note> produced Goethe as their ultimate opposite <note>original: "antithesis"</note>; when the girl who draws lots for her lover is given over to...

<summary>This page concludes the exorcism for reclaiming a soul-contract and explores the theological perspective that even minor superstitions constitute a "tacit pact" with the devil. Waite argues that such extreme religious stances actually encouraged the growth of sorcery by failing to distinguish between harmless folly and serious occultism.</summary>

<keywords>pact with the devil, Lucifuge Rofocale, exorcism, Catholic theology, Bergier, St. Augustine, St. Thomas Aquinas, superstition, Faust, sorcery laws</keywords>

--- Page 216 ---
<meta>This page continues the discussion on the legalistic and theological nature of pacts with the devil, concluding the commentary on the "Vocabulaire Infernal" before introducing the Grimoire of Honorius.</meta>

Satan, the ultimate fate <note>original: apotheosis</note> of Faust is certain. Behind the obvious exaggerations of such definitions, however, lies a hidden grain of truth regarding the recklessness of opening the door to evil; in this sense, even a careless act or a moment of folly carries all its consequences—and everything to which it may lead—implicitly within it.

—in which case Black Magic, with all its grim theatrical displays, is the art of exploiting fallen angels without suffering any consequences. However, the "fallen angel" within the practitioner <note>original: operator</note> would not be exploited so easily in the end.

# CHAPTER VII

## The Method of Honorius

The *Grimoire of Honorius* is perhaps the most openly demonic of all the rituals connected with Black Magic, and yet, as we have already seen, its horrors have been much exaggerated. It compares favorably, for example, with the *Key of Solomon*, and it is preferable to the *Lemegeton* because it is less disguised. Its most obvious problems are: 1. The desecration <note>original: profanation</note> of religious mysteries. 2. A bloody sacrifice, characterized by monstrous details. A less obvious objection is the superstitious nature of some of its rules. It must be admitted that such a charge seems strange, considering the countless useless rituals <note>original: vain observances</note> I have been dealing with. But a useless ritual, it should be remembered, is not necessarily superstitious. To assume that a special power <note>original: virtue</note> lives in parchment prepared from the skin of an animal which has not bred <note>original: engendered</note>, and that such power is missing from the skin of one which has reproduced its species,

<summary>This page concludes a critique on the nature of demonic pacts, highlighting the ease with which some folk traditions claim they can be broken. It then introduces the Grimoire of Honorius, labeling it as openly diabolical yet noting its less-disguised nature compared to other famous magic texts.</summary>

<keywords>Satan, Faust, Lucifer, Grimoire of Honorius, Black Magic, Key of Solomon, Lemegeton, demonic contracts, ritual sacrifice, superstition</keywords>

--- Page 217 ---
<meta>This page continues the analysis of the Grimoire of Honorius, shifting from the general demonic nature of the text to a philosophical discussion on the definition of "superstition" versus "magical theory."</meta>

<image-desc>An occult diagram titled "Mystic figures from the title-page of the 'Grimoire of Honorius'". At the top is a large triangle containing a human-like face above a patriarchal cross (a cross with two horizontal bars). Small symbols are positioned near the vertices of the triangle. Below the triangle are the large serif letters "L" and "M". Underneath these is an oval cartouche containing the letters "M D" surrounded by decorative flourishes. At the very bottom are three circular, swirling sigils.</image-desc>

->MYSTIC FIGURES FROM THE TITLE-PAGE OF THE "GRIMOIRE OF HONORIUS"<-

<page-num>266</page-num>

...may have no foundation in fact, and may be eminently foolish, but it is not a superstitious assumption. It has been well pointed out by Éliphas Lévi <note>A famous 19th-century French occultist and author whose works modernized the study of magic.</note> that the term "superstition" signifies "survival"; that is to say, it is the sign surviving the idea. Thus, the Catholic doctrine of Transubstantiation <note>The belief that the bread and wine of the Eucharist become the literal body and blood of Christ.</note>, in its open theological sense, may or may not be true doctrine, but in neither case can the worship of the consecrated elements <note>The bread and wine used in the sacrament.</note> be superstitious, because such worship draws directly from the idea which has created the outward sign. If a time could be imagined when the Elements should continue to be worshipped after the doctrine itself had passed away, that would be a superstitious observance. 

But the linguistic <note>original: etymological</note> conception of superstition also connects with the idea of redundancy—doing more than is necessary. Thus, a double consecration of the Eucharistic elements would be a superstitious observance, as well as being offensively <note>original: blasphemously</note> absurd, because the first consecration is already effective according to the theological theory <note>original: hypothesis</note>. However, the repetition of the "Hail Mary" <note>original: Angelical Salutation</note> in the devotion of the Holy Rosary is not superstitious, because the first recitation is not necessarily considered effective on its own according to the devotional theory. 

So also, a magical practice which is consistent with the magical theory will not be superstitious, though it may not be consistent with reason. The slaughter of a young goat <note>original: kid</note> with a view to securing "virgin parchment" <note>Parchment made from the skin of a newborn or stillborn animal, considered "pure" for ritual use.</note> is not a superstitious measure, but a precautionary or practical one within that system. The conversion of this slaughter into a sacrifice is not superstitious, because it has a purpose which is consistent with the magical theory: namely, the increase of spiritual power <note>original: virtue</note> by the dedication of the religious motive. However, bringing ceremonies into such a sacrifice that are not precautionary and do not, by the theory, increase the spiritual power, is redundant and therefore superstitious. Such rituals do not occur in the magic books <note>original: Grimoires</note>, with the exception of the *Grimoire of Honorius*, in which the rituals are further characterized

<page-num>267</page-num>

by brutal features. The slaughter of a black rooster <note>original: cock</note> and the removal of the eyes, tongue, and heart—which must be ground into powder, and that powder subsequently sprinkled on the lambskin—is a redundant and monstrous observance. Were I, therefore, an occultist...

<summary>Waite defines superstition as a ritual "sign" that outlives its original meaning or becomes redundant, arguing that while magic may be irrational, it only becomes superstitious when it adds unnecessary, brutal elements—as seen in the Grimoire of Honorius.</summary>
<keywords>Éliphas Lévi, Transubstantiation, superstition, Grimoire of Honorius, black magic, ritual sacrifice, virgin parchment, magical theory</keywords>

--- Page 218 ---
<meta>This page transitions from the theoretical definition of "superstition" to the specific, practical rituals found in the Grimoire of Honorius. It details the preparation required by the practitioner—traditionally a priest—before attempting to summon spirits.</meta>

<page-num>268</page-num>
<page-num>269</page-num>

If I were writing for those with a belief in the effectiveness <note>original: efficacy</note> of magical rituals, I would say that the *Grimoire of Honorius* must be avoided. However, it should be unnecessary today to warn anyone against practices that no one is likely to actually perform. These belong to the foolish mysteries of old, discredited doctrines; they are certainly interesting, but only as curiosities of the past. It should be added that the ritual misuses <note>original: profanations</note> of religious mysteries prescribed by the *Grimoire of Honorius* are not intended as insults to God, and may be forgiven to that extent. Their purpose is not to cause outrage, but to increase the ritual's power.

Having established these initial points, the content of this extremely strange ritual may now be offered to the student in the words of its author. It will be remembered that anyone who desires to invoke the corrupt "Spirits of Darkness" according to the method of Honorius must observe a three-day fast. He must also confess his sins and receive Holy Communion <note>original: approach the Holy Altar</note>. According to the text's logic, he should also be a priest of the Roman Catholic Church <note>original: Latin Church</note>. After these three days, on the following morning at sunrise, he shall recite the Seven Gradual Psalms <note>original: Septem Psalmi Graduales; these are Psalms 120–134, traditionally recited to express penitence and spiritual preparation.</note> along with the accompanying Litanies and Prayers, <gloss>1</gloss> all while on his knees. Furthermore, he must drink no wine and eat no meat on that day. Next, he shall rise at midnight on the first Monday of the month, <gloss>2</gloss> and, since he is apparently a priest, shall perform a Mass of the Holy Spirit. <gloss>3</gloss> <note>original: Missa de Spiritu Sancto; a specific Catholic Mass seeking the guidance and strength of the Holy Spirit.</note>

After the consecration of the bread, taking the Host <note>term: The consecrated bread believed in Catholic theology to become the literal Body of Christ.</note> in his left hand, he shall recite the following prayer on his knees:

### PRAYER

> *My Sovereign Savior Jesus Christ, Son of the living God! You Who for the salvation of all mankind suffered death on the Cross; You Who, before being handed over to Your enemies, by an impulse of inexpressible <note>original: ineffable</note> love, established the Sacrament of Your Body; You Who have granted to us miserable creatures the privilege of making a daily memorial of it; grant to Your unworthy servant—while holding Your Living Body in his hands—all the strength and ability to effectively use that power with which he has been entrusted against the crowd of rebellious spirits. You are their true God, and if they tremble at the sound of Your Name, upon that Holy Name I will call, crying: Jesus Christ! Jesus, be my help, now and forever! Amen.*

After sunrise, a black rooster <note>original: cock</note> must be killed, and the first feather of its left wing must be plucked and saved for use at the proper time. The eyes must be torn out, as well as the tongue and heart; these must be dried in the sun and afterwards ground into a powder. The remains must be buried at sunset in a secret place, and a cross about four inches high <note>original: a palm in height</note> must be placed upon the mound. At each of the four corners, the signs shown below must be drawn with the thumb:

These should be written upon the altar, and at the end of the Mass, the paper should be folded in a new veil of violet silk, to be hidden the next day <note>original: on the morrow</note>, along with the offering <note>original: oblation</note> of the Mass and a portion of the consecrated Host <note>The bread used in the Eucharist, believed by Catholics to be the body of Christ.</note>. <gloss>2</gloss>

<margin>p. 270</margin>

On Thursday evening, the practitioner must rise at midnight. After sprinkling holy water around the room, he must light a candle <note>original: taper</note> made of yellow wax, which must have been prepared on Wednesday and pierced in the shape of a cross. When it is lit, he shall recite Psalm 78 <note>original: Psalm lxxvii; this is numbered according to the Latin Vulgate Bible.</note>—*Hear my law, O my people, etc.* <note>original: Attendite, Popule meus, legem meam, &c.</note>—without the *Glory be to the Father* <note>original: Gloria Patri</note>. He shall then begin the *Office of the Dead* <note>A cycle of prayers for the souls of the deceased.</note> with *Come, let us exult in the Lord, etc.* <note>original: Venite exultemus Domino, &c.</note> He shall recite Matins and Lauds <note>The early morning portions of the divine office.</note>, but instead of the verse <note>original: versicle</note> of the ninth Lesson <gloss>2</gloss> he shall say: 

"Deliver us, O Lord, from the fear of hell. Do not let the demons destroy my soul when I raise them from the deep Pit, when I command them to do my will. May the day be bright, and may the sun and moon shine forth, when I call upon them. They are terrible in appearance, deformed and horrible to see; but You, Lord, restore their angelic forms to them when I impose my will upon them. O Lord, deliver me from those of the terrifying faces, and grant that they shall be obedient when I raise them up from hell, when I impose my will upon them."

After the Office of the Dead, the practitioner shall extinguish the candle. At sunrise, he shall cut the throat of a nine-day-old male lamb, taking care that the blood does not spill onto the ground <note>original: gush forth upon the earth</note>. He shall skin the lamb and throw its tongue and heart into the fire. The fire must be freshly lit, and the ashes shall be kept for use at the appropriate time. The lambskin shall be spread in the middle of a field, and for nine days it shall be sprinkled four times each day with holy water. On the tenth day, before sunrise, the lambskin shall be covered with the

270:1 This Psalm is numbered 78 in the King James Version <note>original: Authorised Version</note>.

270:2 This is extremely confused; the Office of the Dead does not begin with "Come, let us rejoice" <note>original: Venite exultemus</note>. The ninth Lesson belongs to the third Nocturne of the Matins. The prayer given in the Grimoire is based on the verses which it replaces.

271:1 The days on which the various operations should be performed have been confused, following the common habit of the Grimoires.

274:1 At this point, the process described in the Grimoire becomes almost unintelligible. The recitation of the seventy-two names apparently follows the Psalm and is followed by the Second Gospel, which is not omitted after all. There is then an extension of the "Thanks be to God" <note>original: Deo Gratias</note>; this concluded the Mass, which seems to be immediately followed by the evocation. However, the use of the Pentacles of Solomon and St. John does not appear, in one case, until the end of the Conjurations, and in the other case, not at all.

### UNIVERSAL CONJURATION

I, [Name], conjure you, O Spirit [Name], by the living God, by the true God, by the holy and all-ruling God, Who created from nothingness the heaven, the earth, the sea, and all things that are within them; in virtue of the Most Holy Sacrament of the Eucharist, in the name of Jesus Christ, and by the power of this same Almighty Son of God, Who for us and for our redemption was crucified, suffered death, and was buried; Who rose again on the third day and is now seated on the right hand of the Creator of the whole world, from where He will come to judge the living and the dead; as also by the precious love of the Holy Spirit, the Perfect Trinity. I conjure you within the circle, accursed one, by your judgment—you who dared to tempt God. I exorcise you, Serpent, and I command you to appear immediately in a beautiful and pleasing human form of soul and body, and to fulfill my commands without any deceit whatsoever,

*as also without mental reservation of any kind, by the great Names of the God of gods and Lord of lords: Lord <note>original: Adonay</note>, the Four-Lettered Name <note>original: Tetragrammaton</note>, Jehovah, the Four-Lettered Name <note>original: Tetragrammaton</note>, Lord <note>original: Adonay</note>, Jehovah, O God <note>original: Otheos</note>, Immortal <note>original: Athanatos</note>, Mighty <note>original: Ischyros</note>, You are strong forever, O Lord <note>original: AGLA, a Hebrew acronym</note>, the Five-Lettered Name <note>original: Pentagrammaton</note>, Almighty, Almighty, Almighty <note>original: Saday</note>, Jehovah, O God <note>original: Otheos</note>, Immortal <note>original: Athanatos</note>, by the summoning <note>original: à Liciat, likely a corruption of the Latin 'ad liciat'</note>, the Four-Lettered Name <note>original: Tetragrammaton</note>, Lord <note>original: Adonay</note>, Mighty <note>original: Ischyros</note>, Immortal <note>original: Athanatos</note>, Almighty, Almighty, Almighty <note>original: Sady</note>, Holy, Holy, Holy <note>original: Cados</note>, My God <note>original: Eloy</note>, You are strong forever, O Lord, You are strong forever, O Lord, You are strong forever, O Lord <note>original: AGLA</note>, Lord, Lord <note>original: Adonay</note>. I conjure you, evil and accursed Serpent, [Name], to appear at my will and pleasure in this place, before this circle, without delay, without companions, without causing harm, noise, deformity, or complaining. I exorcise you by the ineffable Names of God, namely, Gog and Magog, which I am unworthy to pronounce: Come here, Come here, Come here. Accomplish my will and desire, without trickery or falsehood. Otherwise, St. Michael, the invisible Archangel, shall immediately strike you into the utmost depths of hell. Come, then, [Name], to do my will.*

### DISCHARGE

*Go in peace to your places. May there be peace between us and you, and be ready to come when you are called. In the Name of the Father, and of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit <note>original: Ghost</note>. Amen.*

### ACT OF THANKSGIVING

*Praise, honor, glory, and blessing be to Him who sits upon the throne, who lives for ever and ever. Amen.*

*...of the Most Holy Trinity, in the Name of the Most Holy Trinity, in the Name of the Most Holy Trinity!*

*What follows must be said before the sealing of the Book:*

*I summon and command you, O Spirits, as many as you are, to accept this Book willingly, so that whenever we may read it, the book being approved and recognized as proper and valid, you shall be compelled to appear in a pleasant human form when you are called, according to the reader's judgment. Under no circumstances shall you attack the body, soul, or spirit of the reader, nor inflict any harm on those who may accompany him, whether by mutterings, storms, noise, or scandals, nor by physical injury <note>original: lesion</note> or by hindering the execution of the commands of this Book. I summon you to appear immediately when the conjuration is made, to execute without delay all that is written and listed in its proper place in this book. You shall obey, serve, instruct, impart, and perform everything in your power for the benefit of those who command you, and all of this without deception <note>original: illusion</note>. If by chance some of the summoned spirits are unable to come or appear when required, they shall be obligated to send others invested with their power, who shall also swear solemnly to execute all that the reader may demand; and you are all hereby joined by the Most Holy Names of the Almighty Living God, **ELOYM**, **JAH**, **EL**, **ELOY**, **TETRAGRAMMATON** <note>These are various Hebrew names for God used in magical rituals to exert authority over spirits</note>, to fulfill everything as it is set forth above. If you do not obey me, I will force you to remain in torments for a thousand years, as I will also do if any one of you does not receive this Book with entire resignation to the will of the reader.*

### CONCERNING THE DESIGN OF THE CIRCLE

Circles should be drawn <note>original: described</note> with charcoal or holy water, and sprinkled with a branch of the blessed Cross. <note>1</note> When they have been properly made and the sacred words have been written around the circle, the holy water which was used to bless it may also be used to prevent the spirits from inflicting any harm. Standing in the middle of the circle, you shall command them forcefully <note>original: in a lively manner</note>, as one who is their master.

### WHAT MUST BE SAID IN COMPOSING THE CIRCLE

> *O Lord, we flee to Your power! O Lord, strengthen this work! Let what is performed within us become like dust driven before the wind; and as the Angel of the Lord stands by <note>The original text includes "(sic)" here, suggesting a potential error in the source manuscript regarding the word "pausing"</note>, let the darkness disappear; and with the Angel of the Lord ever pursuing: ALPHA, OMEGA, ELY, ELOHE, ELOHIM, ZEBAOTH <note>original: ZABAHOT; Hebrew for "of hosts"</note>, ELYON <note>original: ELION; Hebrew for "Most High"</note>, SHADDAI <note>original: SADY; Hebrew for "Almighty"</note>.*

---

> *Behold the Lion who is the conqueror of the Tribe of Judah, the Root of David! I will open the Book and its seven seals. I have seen Satan falling like a lightning bolt from heaven. It is You who have given us power to crush dragons, scorpions, and all Your enemies beneath Your feet. Nothing shall harm us, not even by the names: ELOY, ELOHIM, ELOHE, ZEBAOTH, ELYON, ESARCHIE <note>Esarchie is likely a corruption of a Greek or Hebrew divine title</note>.*

### THE MAGIC CIRCLE OF HONORIUS

ADONAY <note>Hebrew: "My Lord"</note>, JAH <note>A shortened form of the divine name Yahweh</note>, TETRAGRAMMATON <note>The sacred four-letter name of God, YHVH</note>, SADY <note>original: Shaddai; Hebrew for "Almighty"</note>. *The earth is the Lord's and all those who dwell therein, because He established it upon the seas and prepared it in the midst of the waves. Who shall ascend to the mountain of the Lord? Who shall be received in His Holy Place? The one with innocent hands and a clean heart. One who has not committed his soul to vanity <note>original: "received his soul in vain"</note> and has not sworn false witness against his neighbor. This person shall be blessed by God and shall obtain mercy from God for his salvation. He belongs to the generation of those who seek Him.* <note>This text is a translation of Psalm 24.</note>

*Open your gates, you princes; open the eternal gates, and the King of Glory shall enter! Who is this King of Glory? The Lord Almighty, the Lord, mighty in battle. Open your gates, you Princes! Lift up the eternal gates. Who is this King of Glory? The Lord Almighty. This Lord is the King of Glory. Glory be to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit.* <note>The concluding line is the "Gloria Patri," a common Christian doxology.</note>

## A LESSER PENTACLE OF SOLOMON

*To dismiss the spirits, the Pentacle of Solomon must be displayed while saying the following:*

> Behold your sentence! Behold that which forbids rebellion against our wills and ordains you to return to your dwellings. May peace be between us and you; be ready to come, each and all, whenever you are called to do my will.

## Footnotes

280:1 Based on the theory that the *Method of Honorius* <note>original: A reference to the Grimoire of Honorius, a famous book of black magic</note> provides effective rituals as long as its conditions are met, the operation must fail because this specific requirement is unattainable, if not actually impossible. I mention this for the benefit of those practitioners of the occult who might believe in its effectiveness. I do not believe such people exist, but since there is no limit to the foolishness found in the occult, it remains within the realm of a dream.

## CONJURATION OF THE KING OF THE EAST

*I conjure and invoke you, O powerful King of the East, MAGOA <note>The demonic king governing the eastern quarter of the world</note>, by my holy labor, by all the names of Divinity, and by the name of the All-Powerful: I command you to obey and to come to me. If you fail, send MASSAYEL, ARIEL, SATIEL, ARDUEL, and ACORIB at once to answer everything I wish to know and to fulfill everything I shall command. Otherwise, you shall surely come in your own person to satisfy my will; if you refuse, I shall compel you by all the virtue and power of God.*

The Grand Pentacle or Circle of Solomon will suffice for the conjuration above and those that follow, which can be performed on all days and at all hours. If you wish to speak with only one spirit, only one needs to be named, at the choice of the reader.

### CONJURATION OF THE KING OF THE SOUTH

*O EGYM <note>Egym is identified in various grimoires as a powerful demonic king ruling the southern quarter of the world.</note>, great King of the South, I summon and invoke you by the most high and holy names of God. Reveal yourself here, clothed in all your power; come before this circle, or at least send me immediately Fadal and Nastrache to answer me and to carry out all my wishes. If you fail, I shall compel you by God Himself.*

### CONJURATION OF THE KING OF THE WEST

*O BAYMON, most powerful King who reigns in the Western quarter, I call upon and invoke you in the name of the Deity! I command you by the power of the Most High to send the spirit Passiel Rosus immediately before this circle, along with all other spirits who are subject to you, so that they may answer everything just as I require of them. If you fail, I will torment you with the divine sword of fire; I will multiply your sufferings and burn you.*

### CONJURATION OF THE KING OF THE NORTH

*O you, AMAYMON, King and Emperor of the Northern regions, I call, invoke, exorcise, and command you, by the virtue and power of the Creator, and by the highest of all powers <note>original: "virtue of virtues"</note>, to send me now and without delay Madael, Laaval, Bamlahe, Belem, and Ramath, with all other spirits under your command, in a handsome and human form! In whatever place you are now, come here and give the honor you owe to the true living God, who is your Creator. In the name of the Father, of the Son, and of the Holy Spirit, come therefore and be obedient in front of this circle, without danger to my body or soul. Appear in a handsome human form, with no terror surrounding you. I command you: make haste, come straight away and at once. By all the divine names—SECHIEL, BARACHIEL—if you do not obey promptly, I invoke the Avenger <note>original: Balandier</note>, the suspended one <note>original: suspensus</note>, the wrathful one <note>original: iracundus</note>, Origratiumgu, Partus, Olemdemis, and Bautratis, N.*

*I exorcise you, I invoke you, and I impose this most high commandment upon you by the omnipotence of the living God and of the true God; by the power of the holy God and by the power of Him who spoke and all things were made—even by His holy commandment the heavens and earth were created, with everything in them! I command you by the Father, by the Son, and by the Holy Spirit, even by the Holy Trinity, by that God whom you cannot resist and under whose authority I will compel you. I conjure you by God the Father, by God the Son, by God the Holy Spirit, by the Mother of Jesus Christ, Holy Mother and Perpetual Virgin, by her sacred heart, and by her blessed milk which the Son of the Father sucked; by her most holy body and soul, by all the parts and members of this Virgin, and by all the sufferings, afflictions, labors, and agonies which she endured during the whole course of her life; by all the sighs she uttered, and by all the holy tears which she shed while her dear Son wept before—*

<page-num>285</page-num>

*—afflictions, labors, and agonies which she endured during the whole course of her life, by all the sighs she uttered, and by all the holy tears which she shed while her dear Son wept before...*

In the same way, I conjure you by the Holy Trinity, by all other mysteries, by the sign of the Cross, by the most precious blood and water which flowed from the side of Jesus Christ, and by the sweat which came from His whole body when He said in the Garden of Olives: "My Father, if it be possible, let this cup <note>original: "chalice"</note> pass from me." I conjure you by the judgment of the living and the dead, by the Gospel words of our Savior Jesus Christ, by His preachings, by His sayings, by His miracles, by the child in swaddling clothes, and by the crying child carried by the mother in her most pure and virginal womb; by the glorious intercession of the Virgin Mother of our Savior Jesus Christ; by all which is of God and of His Most Holy Mother, both in heaven and on earth. 

I conjure you by the holy Angels and Archangels, by all the blessed orders of Spirits, by the holy patriarchs and Prophets, by all the holy martyrs and confessors, by all the holy virgins and innocent widows, and by all the saints of God, both men and women. I conjure you by the head of St. John the Baptist, by the milk of St. Catherine <note>A reference to the legend of St. Catherine of Alexandria, where milk supposedly flowed from her neck instead of blood upon her decapitation</note>, and by all the Saints.

**Items needed:** coal and consecrated chalk to draw the circle. Around the circle, these words must be written: *I forbid you, Lucifer, in the name of the Most Holy Trinity, to enter within this circle.* A mouse must be provided as an offering for him. The master of the ritual must wear a stole <note>A long, scarf-like cloth worn by clergy</note> and have holy water, as well as an alb <note>A white, ankle-length robe</note> and a surplice <note>A loose white vestment worn over a robe</note>. He must recite the Summoning <note>original: "Conjuration"</note> in an energetic manner, commanding sharply and briefly, as a lord would address his servant, <note>In ceremonial magic, the practitioner does not ask favors but commands spirits as a superior officer would</note> using all kinds of threats: SATAN, RANTAM, PALLANTRE, LUTAIS, CORICACOEM, SCIRCIGREUR, I require you to serve me very humbly, etc.

He must also be powerless to hurt me, or any person whatsoever, in either body or soul.*
